                  END ELSE BEGIN
                  //LS +
                    DimMgt.MoveOneDocDimToPostedDocDim(
                      TempDocDim,DATABASE::"Sales Header","Document Type","No.",0,
                      DATABASE::"Sales Shipment Header",SalesShptHeader."No.");
                  END;  //LS
                  ApprovalMgt.MoveApprvalEntryToPosted(TempApprovalEntry,DATABASE::"Sales Shipment Header",SalesShptHeader."No.");

                  IF SalesSetup."Copy Comments Order to Shpt." THEN BEGIN
                    CopyCommentLines(
                      "Document Type",SalesCommentLine."Document Type"::Shipment,
                      "No.",SalesShptHeader."No.");
                    SalesShptHeader.COPYLINKS(Rec);
                  END;
                  IF WhseShip THEN BEGIN
                    WhseShptHeader.GET(TempWhseShptHeader."No.");
                    WhsePostShpt.CreatePostedShptHeader(
                      PostedWhseShptHeader,WhseShptHeader,"Shipping No.","Posting Date");
                  END;
                  IF WhseReceive THEN BEGIN
                    WhseRcptHeader.GET(TempWhseRcptHeader."No.");
                    WhsePostRcpt.CreatePostedRcptHeader(
                      PostedWhseRcptHeader,WhseRcptHeader,"Shipping No.","Posting Date");
                  END;
                END;

                ServItemMgt.CopyReservationEntry(SalesHeader);

                IF ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::Invoice) AND
                   (NOT SalesSetup."Shipment on Invoice")
                THEN
                  ServItemMgt.CreateServItemOnSalesInvoice(SalesHeader);
              END;

              // Insert return receipt header
              IF Receive THEN
                IF ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Return Order") OR
                   // --> 20160602-APP21-TER01.01.00-2636-OAN01
                   // BEFORE
                   {(("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo") AND SalesSetup."Return Receipt on Credit Memo")}
                   // AFTER
                   (("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo") AND SalesSetup."Return Receipt on Credit Memo") OR
                   (("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo") AND ("Annulment Doc. No." <> ''))
                   // <-- 20160602-APP21-TER01.01.00-2636-OAN01
                THEN BEGIN
                  ReturnRcptHeader.INIT;
                  ReturnRcptHeader.TRANSFERFIELDS(SalesHeader);
                  // --> 20160602-APP21-TER01.01.00-2636-OAN01
                  IF SalesHeader."Dist. Shpt Posting Date" <> 0D THEN
                    ReturnRcptHeader."Posting Date" := SalesHeader."Dist. Shpt Posting Date";
                  // <-- 20160602-APP21-TER01.01.00-2636-OAN01
                  ReturnRcptHeader."No." := "Return Receipt No.";
                  IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Return Order" THEN BEGIN
                    ReturnRcptHeader."Return Order No. Series" := "No. Series";
                    ReturnRcptHeader."Return Order No." := "No.";
                    IF SalesSetup."Ext. Doc. No. Mandatory" THEN
                      TESTFIELD("External Document No.");
                  END;
                  ReturnRcptHeader."No. Series" := "Return Receipt No. Series";
                  ReturnRcptHeader."Source Code" := SrcCode;
                  ReturnRcptHeader."User ID" := USERID;
                  ReturnRcptHeader."No. Printed" := 0;
                  // --> 20150506-APP36-TER01.01.00-269-BSI01
                  ReturnRcptHeader."Assigned User ID" := TempWhseRcptHeader."Assigned User ID";
                  // <-- 20150506-APP36-TER01.01.00-269-BSI01
                  // --> 20161230-LogA07-SCT00.00.00-3208-NED
                  ReturnRcptHeader."Warehouse Receipt No." := TempWhseRcptHeader."No.";
                  ReturnRcptHeader."Terminal Scan Error" := TempWhseRcptHeader."Terminal Scan Error";
                  // <-- 20161230-LogA07-SCT00.00.00-3208-NED

                  // -->20141127-WI00-CMC02.02.00-FBE01
                  ReturnRcptHeader."Created Date" := CREATEDATETIME(TODAY,TIME);
                  // <-- 20141127-WI00-CMC02.02.00-FBE01
                  // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                  // BEFORE
                  //ReturnRcptHeader.INSERT(TRUE);
                  // AFTER
                  IF NOT G_isSalesCollection THEN
                    ReturnRcptHeader.INSERT(TRUE)
                  ELSE BEGIN
                    WhseReceive := FALSE;
                    WhseShip := FALSE;
                  END;
                  // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04

                  //LS -
                  IF SalesHeader."Only Two Dimensions" THEN BEGIN
                    TempDocDim.SETRANGE(TempDocDim."Table ID",DATABASE::"Sales Header");
                    TempDocDim.SETRANGE(TempDocDim."Line No.",0);
                    DimMgt.MoveDocDimToPostedDocDim(TempDocDim,DATABASE::"Return Receipt Header",ReturnRcptHeader."No.");
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                  //LS +
                    DimMgt.MoveOneDocDimToPostedDocDim(
                      TempDocDim,DATABASE::"Sales Header","Document Type","No.",0,
                      DATABASE::"Return Receipt Header",ReturnRcptHeader."No.");
                  END;  //LS

                  ApprovalMgt.MoveApprvalEntryToPosted(TempApprovalEntry,DATABASE::"Return Receipt Header",ReturnRcptHeader."No.");

                  IF SalesSetup."Copy Cmts Ret.Ord. to Ret.Rcpt" THEN BEGIN
                    CopyCommentLines(
                      "Document Type",SalesCommentLine."Document Type"::"Posted Return Receipt",
                      "No.",ReturnRcptHeader."No.");
                    ReturnRcptHeader.COPYLINKS(Rec);
                  END;
                  IF WhseReceive THEN BEGIN
                    WhseRcptHeader.GET(TempWhseRcptHeader."No.");
                    WhsePostRcpt.CreatePostedRcptHeader(PostedWhseRcptHeader,WhseRcptHeader,"Return Receipt No.","Posting Date");
                  END;
                  IF WhseShip THEN BEGIN
                    WhseShptHeader.GET(TempWhseShptHeader."No.");
                    WhsePostShpt.CreatePostedShptHeader(PostedWhseShptHeader,WhseShptHeader,"Return Receipt No.","Posting Date");
                  END;
                END;
              // --> 20160330-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2882-JCD01
              DueDate_G := SalesHeader."Due Date";
              // <-- 20160330-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2882-JCD01
              // Insert invoice header or credit memo header
              IF Invoice THEN
                IF "Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::Order,"Document Type"::Invoice] THEN BEGIN
                  SalesInvHeader.INIT;
                  SalesInvHeader.TRANSFERFIELDS(SalesHeader);

                  SalesInvHeader."Date Sent" := 0D;
                  SalesInvHeader."Time Sent" := 0T;

                  IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::Order THEN BEGIN
                    SalesInvHeader."No." := "Posting No.";
                    IF SalesSetup."Ext. Doc. No. Mandatory" THEN
                      // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                      IF NOT G_isSalesCollection THEN
                      // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                        TESTFIELD("External Document No.");
                    SalesInvHeader."Pre-Assigned No. Series" := '';
                    SalesInvHeader."Order No. Series" := "No. Series";
                    SalesInvHeader."Order No." := "No.";
                    IF ShowDialog THEN  //LS
                      Window.UPDATE(1,STRSUBSTNO(Text007,"Document Type","No.",SalesInvHeader."No."));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SalesInvHeader."Pre-Assigned No. Series" := "No. Series";
                    SalesInvHeader."Pre-Assigned No." := "No.";
                    IF "Posting No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
                      SalesInvHeader."No." := "Posting No.";
                      IF ShowDialog THEN  //LS
                        Window.UPDATE(1,STRSUBSTNO(Text007,"Document Type","No.",SalesInvHeader."No."));
                    END;
                  END;
                  IF G_cduLSRetailPermission.isLSRetail THEN BEGIN // 20110801-CMC01.05.00-DRN
                    //LS -
                    IF SalesHeader."Source Code" <> '' THEN
                      SalesInvHeader."Source Code" := SalesHeader."Source Code"
                    ELSE
                    //LS +
                      SalesInvHeader."Source Code" := SrcCode;
                    //LS -
                    SalesHeader.CALCFIELDS("Order Amount", "Payment-At Order Entry-Limit", "Payment-At Delivery-Limit",
                                           "Retail Zones Description", "Non Refund Amount",
                                           "Payment-At PurchaseOrder-Limit");
                    SalesInvHeader."Order Amount" := SalesHeader."Order Amount";
                    SalesInvHeader."Payment-At Order Entry-Limit" := SalesHeader."Payment-At Order Entry-Limit";
                    SalesInvHeader."Payment-At Delivery-Limit" := SalesHeader."Payment-At Delivery-Limit";
                    SalesInvHeader."Retail Zones Description" := SalesHeader."Retail Zones Description";
                    SalesInvHeader."Non Refund Amount" := SalesHeader."Non Refund Amount";
                    SalesInvHeader."Payment-At PurchaseOrder-Limit" := SalesHeader."Payment-At PurchaseOrder-Limit";
                    //LS +
                  END ELSE // 20110801-CMC01.05.00-DRN
                    SalesInvHeader."Source Code" := SrcCode;

                  // --> 20140722-APP39-CMC02.02.00-FEC02
                  PrepLogMgt_L.FlushPrepDedLogEntryToPost(SalesInvHeader."No.",TmpPrepLogEntry_G);
                  // --< 20140722-APP39-CMC02.02.00-FEC02

                  SalesInvHeader."User ID" := USERID;
                  SalesInvHeader."No. Printed" := 0;
                  // --> 20140925-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01
                  IF SalesInvHeader."To Distribute" THEN BEGIN
                    SalesInvHeader."Distributed SO No.":='';
                    SalesInvHeader.Distributed:=FALSE;
                  END;
                  // <-- 20140925-APP21-TER01.01.00-PDC01
                  // --> 20141010-CER73-TER01.01.00-PSC01
                  cuPostFarmerMgt_L.UpdateSalesInvHeaDoc(SalesInvHeader);
                  // <-- 20141010-CER73-TER01.01.00-PSC01
                  SalesInvHeader.INSERT;

                  // --> 20140925-APP19-CMC02.02.00-FEC01
                  SalesPriceDiscMgt_G.MoveSalesDiscBuffToPost(SalesHeader,0,SalesInvHeader."No.");
                  // <-- 20140925-APP19-CMC02.02.00-FEC01

                  // --> 20141218-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
                  // --> 20150115-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
                  // Before
                  //UpdateReferencePrice(SalesInvHeader."Sales Contract No.");
                  // After
                  UpdateReferencePrice(SalesInvHeader."Sales Contract No.",SalesInvHeader."No.");
                  // <-- 20150115-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
                  // <-- 20141218-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01

                  CopyRetailComments(DATABASE::"Sales Header","No.",DATABASE::"Sales Invoice Header",SalesInvHeader."No."); //LS6.20.01-01

                  IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::Order THEN BEGIN
                    Opp.RESET;
                    Opp.SETCURRENTKEY("Sales Document Type","Sales Document No.");
                    Opp.SETRANGE("Sales Document Type",Opp."Sales Document Type"::Order);
                    Opp.SETRANGE("Sales Document No.","No.");
                    Opp.SETRANGE(Status,Opp.Status::Won);
                    IF Opp.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                      Opp."Sales Document Type" := Opp."Sales Document Type"::"Posted Invoice";
                      Opp."Sales Document No." := SalesInvHeader."No.";
                      Opp.MODIFY;
                      OpportunityEntry.RESET;
                      OpportunityEntry.SETCURRENTKEY(Active,"Opportunity No.");
                      OpportunityEntry.SETRANGE(Active,TRUE);
                      OpportunityEntry.SETRANGE("Opportunity No.",Opp."No.");
                      IF OpportunityEntry.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                        OpportunityEntry."Calcd. Current Value (LCY)" := OpportunityEntry.GetSalesDocValue(Rec);
                        OpportunityEntry.MODIFY;
                      END;
                    END;
                  END;

                  //LS -
                  IF SalesHeader."Only Two Dimensions" THEN BEGIN
                    TempDocDim.SETRANGE(TempDocDim."Table ID",DATABASE::"Sales Header");
                    TempDocDim.SETRANGE(TempDocDim."Line No.",0);
                    DimMgt.MoveDocDimToPostedDocDim(TempDocDim,DATABASE::"Sales Invoice Header",SalesInvHeader."No.");
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                  //LS +
                    DimMgt.MoveOneDocDimToPostedDocDim(
                      TempDocDim,DATABASE::"Sales Header","Document Type","No.",0,
                      DATABASE::"Sales Invoice Header",SalesInvHeader."No.");
                  END;  //LS

                  ApprovalMgt.MoveApprvalEntryToPosted(TempApprovalEntry,DATABASE::"Sales Invoice Header",SalesInvHeader."No.");

                  IF SalesSetup."Copy Comments Order to Invoice" THEN BEGIN
                    CopyCommentLines(
                      "Document Type",SalesCommentLine."Document Type"::"Posted Invoice",
                      "No.",SalesInvHeader."No.");
                    SalesInvHeader.COPYLINKS(Rec);
                  END;
                  GenJnlLineDocType := GenJnlLine."Document Type"::Invoice;
                  GenJnlLineDocNo := SalesInvHeader."No.";
                  GenJnlLineExtDocNo := SalesInvHeader."External Document No.";

                END ELSE BEGIN // Credit Memo
                  SalesCrMemoHeader.INIT;
                  SalesCrMemoHeader.TRANSFERFIELDS(SalesHeader);
                  IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Return Order" THEN BEGIN
                    SalesCrMemoHeader."No." := "Posting No.";
                    IF SalesSetup."Ext. Doc. No. Mandatory" THEN
                      TESTFIELD("External Document No.");
                    SalesCrMemoHeader."Pre-Assigned No. Series" := '';
                    SalesCrMemoHeader."Return Order No. Series" := "No. Series";
                    SalesCrMemoHeader."Return Order No." := "No.";
                    IF ShowDialog THEN  //LS
                      Window.UPDATE(1,STRSUBSTNO(Text008,"Document Type","No.",SalesCrMemoHeader."No."))
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SalesCrMemoHeader."Pre-Assigned No. Series" := "No. Series";
                    SalesCrMemoHeader."Pre-Assigned No." := "No.";
                    IF "Posting No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
                      SalesCrMemoHeader."No." := "Posting No.";
                      IF ShowDialog THEN  //LS
                        Window.UPDATE(1,STRSUBSTNO(Text008,"Document Type","No.",SalesCrMemoHeader."No."));
                    END;
                  END;
                  SalesCrMemoHeader."Source Code" := SrcCode;
                  SalesCrMemoHeader."User ID" := USERID;
                  SalesCrMemoHeader."No. Printed" := 0;
                  // --> 20141010-CER73-TER01.01.00-PSC01
                  cuPostFarmerMgt_L.UpdateSalesCrMemoHeaDoc(SalesCrMemoHeader);
                  // <-- 20141010-CER73-TER01.01.00-PSC01
                  SalesCrMemoHeader.INSERT;

                  // --> 20140925-APP19-CMC02.02.00-FEC01
                  SalesPriceDiscMgt_G.MoveSalesDiscBuffToPost(SalesHeader,0,SalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
                  // <-- 20140925-APP19-CMC02.02.00-FEC01

                  // --> 20141218-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
                  // --> 20150115-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
                  // Before
                  //UpdateReferencePrice(SalesCrMemoHeader."Sales Contract No.");
                  // After
                  UpdateReferencePrice(SalesCrMemoHeader."Sales Contract No.",SalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
                  // <-- 20150115-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
                  // <-- 20141218-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01

                  //LS -
                  IF SalesHeader."Only Two Dimensions" THEN BEGIN
                    TempDocDim.SETRANGE(TempDocDim."Table ID",DATABASE::"Sales Header");
                    TempDocDim.SETRANGE(TempDocDim."Line No.",0);
                    DimMgt.MoveDocDimToPostedDocDim(TempDocDim,DATABASE::"Sales Cr.Memo Header",SalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                  //LS +
                    DimMgt.MoveOneDocDimToPostedDocDim(
                      TempDocDim,DATABASE::"Sales Header","Document Type","No.",0,
                      DATABASE::"Sales Cr.Memo Header",SalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
                  END;  //LS

                  ApprovalMgt.MoveApprvalEntryToPosted(TempApprovalEntry,DATABASE::"Sales Cr.Memo Header",SalesCrMemoHeader."No.");

                  IF SalesSetup."Copy Cmts Ret.Ord. to Cr. Memo" THEN BEGIN
                    CopyCommentLines(
                      "Document Type",SalesCommentLine."Document Type"::"Posted Credit Memo",
                      "No.",SalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
                    SalesCrMemoHeader.COPYLINKS(Rec);
                  END;
                  GenJnlLineDocType := GenJnlLine."Document Type"::"Credit Memo";
                  GenJnlLineDocNo := SalesCrMemoHeader."No.";
                  GenJnlLineExtDocNo := SalesCrMemoHeader."External Document No.";
                END;

              // Lines
              InvPostingBuffer[1].DELETEALL;
              DropShipPostBuffer.DELETEALL;
              EverythingInvoiced := TRUE;

              SalesLine.RESET;
              SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
              SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
              // --> 20140309-8060990/104-CMB6.00.10.09-1815-FEC02
              IF "Post Drop Shipment Only" THEN
                SalesLine.SETRANGE("Drop Shipment",TRUE);
              // <-- 20140309-8060990/104-CMB6.00.10.09-1815-FEC02

              LineCount := 0;
              RoundingLineInserted := FALSE;
              MergeSaleslines(SalesHeader,SalesLine,TempPrepaymentSalesLine,CombinedSalesLineTemp);

              TempVATAmountLineRemainder.DELETEALL;
              SalesLine.CalcVATAmountLines(1,SalesHeader,CombinedSalesLineTemp,TempVATAmountLine);

              // --> 20120624-Log03-CMC02.01.00-SD01
              TempSalesLine_G.DELETEALL;
              // <-- 20120624-Log03-CMC02.01.00-SD01

              IF SalesLine.FINDSET THEN
                REPEAT
                  // --> 20130726-Gen12-CMC02.02.00-BRA01
                  IF ((SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::Item) AND (SalesLine."Document Type"=SalesLine."Document Type"::Order)) THEN
                    BlockingStatusMgt_L.CheckBlockingSalesLine(SalesLine."No.",SalesHeader."Bill-to Customer No.",Ship,Invoice);
                  // <-- 20130726-Gen12-CMC02.02.00-BRA01

                  ItemJnlRollRndg := FALSE;
                  LineCount := LineCount + 1;
                  IF ShowDialog THEN  //LS
                    Window.UPDATE(2,LineCount);
                  IF SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::"Charge (Item)" THEN BEGIN
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD(Amount);
                    // --> 20140128-ANI01-CMC02.02.00-1350-BSA01
                    IF SalesLine."Job No." <> '' THEN
                      RecJob_L.GET(SalesLine."Job No.");
                    IF NOT RecJob_L.Breeding THEN BEGIN
                    // <-- 20140128-ANI01-CMC02.02.00-1350-BSA01
                      SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Job No.",'');
                      SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Job Contract Entry No.",0);
                    // --> 20140128-ANI01-CMC02.02.00-1350-BSA01
                    END;
                    // <-- 20140128-ANI01-CMC02.02.00-1350-BSA01
                  END;
                  IF SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::Item THEN
                    CostBaseAmount := SalesLine."Line Amount";
                  IF SalesLine."Qty. per Unit of Measure" = 0 THEN
                    SalesLine."Qty. per Unit of Measure" := 1;
                  CASE "Document Type" OF
                    "Document Type"::Order:
                      SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Return Qty. to Receive",0);
                    "Document Type"::Invoice:
                      BEGIN
                        IF SalesLine."Shipment No." = '' THEN
                          SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Qty. to Ship",SalesLine.Quantity);

                        // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                        IF G_isSalesCollection THEN
                          IF SalesLine."Posted Delivery No." = '' THEN
                            SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Qty. to Ship",SalesLine.Quantity);
                        // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04

                        SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Return Qty. to Receive",0);
                        SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Qty. to Invoice",SalesLine.Quantity);
                      END;
                    "Document Type"::"Return Order":
                      SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Qty. to Ship",0);
                    "Document Type"::"Credit Memo":
                      BEGIN
                        IF SalesLine."Return Receipt No." = '' THEN
                          SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Return Qty. to Receive",SalesLine.Quantity);

                        // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                        IF G_isSalesCollection THEN
                          IF SalesLine."Posted Delivery No." = '' THEN
                            SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Return Qty. to Receive",SalesLine.Quantity);
                        // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04

                        SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Qty. to Ship",0);
                        SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Qty. to Invoice",SalesLine.Quantity);
                      END;
                  END;

                  IF NOT (Ship OR RoundingLineInserted) THEN BEGIN
                    SalesLine."Qty. to Ship" := 0;
                    SalesLine."Qty. to Ship (Base)" := 0;
                  END;
                  IF NOT (Receive OR RoundingLineInserted) THEN BEGIN
                    SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive" := 0;
                    SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive (Base)" := 0;
                  END;

                  JobContractLine := FALSE;
                  TempJnlLineDim2.RESET;
                  TempJnlLineDim2.DELETEALL;
                  TempDocDim.RESET;
                  TempDocDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Sales Line");
                  TempDocDim.SETRANGE("Line No.",SalesLine."Line No.");
                  DimMgt.CopyDocDimToJnlLineDim(TempDocDim,TempJnlLineDim2);
                  IF TempJnlLineDim2.FIND('-') THEN;
                  IF (SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::Item) OR
                     (SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::"G/L Account")  OR
                     (SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::" ")
                  THEN
                    // --> 20140116-ANI01-CMC02.02.00-903-BSA01
                    IF Invoice THEN
                    // <-- 20140116-ANI01-CMC02.02.00-903-BSA01
                      IF SalesLine."Job Contract Entry No." > 0 THEN
                        PostJobContractLine(SalesLine,TempJnlLineDim2);
                  IF (SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::Resource) THEN
                    JobTaskSalesLine := SalesLine;

                  IF SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::"Fixed Asset" THEN BEGIN
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Job No.",'');
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Depreciation Book Code");
                    DeprBook.GET(SalesLine."Depreciation Book Code");
                    DeprBook.TESTFIELD("G/L Integration - Disposal",TRUE);
                    FA.GET(SalesLine."No.");
                    FA.TESTFIELD("Budgeted Asset",FALSE);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Depreciation Book Code",'');
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Depr. until FA Posting Date",FALSE);
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD("FA Posting Date",0D);
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Duplicate in Depreciation Book",'');
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Use Duplication List",FALSE);
                  END;

                  IF ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::Invoice) AND (SalesLine."Shipment No." <> '') THEN BEGIN
                    SalesLine."Quantity Shipped" := SalesLine.Quantity;
                    SalesLine."Qty. Shipped (Base)" := SalesLine."Quantity (Base)";
                    SalesLine."Qty. to Ship" := 0;
                    SalesLine."Qty. to Ship (Base)" := 0;
                  END;

                  // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                  IF G_isSalesCollection THEN BEGIN
                    IF ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::Invoice) AND (SalesLine."Posted Delivery No." <> '') THEN BEGIN
                      SalesLine."Quantity Shipped" := SalesLine.Quantity;
                      SalesLine."Qty. Shipped (Base)" := SalesLine."Quantity (Base)";
                      SalesLine."Qty. to Ship" := 0;
                      SalesLine."Qty. to Ship (Base)" := 0;
                    END;
                    // --> 20140128-Cer17-CMC02.02.00-1333-SDE01
                    IF ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::Invoice) AND (SalesLine."Collection Recept No." <> '') THEN BEGIN
                      SalesLine."Quantity Shipped" := SalesLine.Quantity;
                      SalesLine."Qty. Shipped (Base)" := SalesLine."Quantity (Base)";
                      SalesLine."Qty. to Ship" := 0;
                      SalesLine."Qty. to Ship (Base)" := 0;
                    END;
                    // <-- 20140128-Cer17-CMC02.02.00-1333-SDE01
                  END;
                  // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                  // --> 20160526-GEN04-CMC02.02.01-2905-NED01
                  //IF ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo") AND (SalesLine."Return Receipt No." <> '') THEN BEGIN
                  IF ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo") AND ((SalesLine."Return Receipt No." <> '') OR
                       (SalesLine."Collection Recept No." <> '')) THEN BEGIN
                  // <-- 20160526-GEN04-CMC02.02.01-2905-NED01
                    SalesLine."Return Qty. Received" := SalesLine.Quantity;
                    SalesLine."Return Qty. Received (Base)" := SalesLine."Quantity (Base)";
                    SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive" := 0;
                    SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive (Base)" := 0;
                  END;

                  // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                  IF G_isSalesCollection THEN BEGIN
                    // --> 20160526-GEN04-CMC02.02.01-2905-NED01
                    //IF ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo") AND (SalesLine."Return Receipt No." <> '') THEN BEGIN
                    IF ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo") AND ((SalesLine."Return Receipt No." <> '') OR
                         (SalesLine."Collection Recept No." <> '')) THEN BEGIN
                    // <-- 20160526-GEN04-CMC02.02.01-2905-NED01
                      SalesLine."Return Qty. Received" := SalesLine.Quantity;
                      SalesLine."Return Qty. Received (Base)" := SalesLine."Quantity (Base)";
                      SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive" := 0;
                      SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive (Base)" := 0;
                    END;
                  END;
                  // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04

                  // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                  // -----------------------------------------------------------
                  // Gestion Solder le Reliquat
                  // -----------------------------------------------------------
                  IF SalesLine.Quantity <> 0 THEN
                    // --> 20130919-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-0817-FBE
                    IF SalesLine."Cancel Remaining Qty" AND ("Document Type" <> "Document Type"::"Credit Memo")  THEN
                    // <-- 20130919-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-0817-FBE
                      IF ((SalesLine."Qty. to Ship" + SalesLine."Quantity Shipped") < SalesLine.Quantity) THEN
                        L_cduWhsePostShipment.UpdateQtyOfSalesLine(SalesLine."Qty. to Ship", SalesLine);
                  // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04

                  IF Invoice THEN BEGIN
                    IF ABS(SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice") > ABS(SalesLine.MaxQtyToInvoice) THEN
                    // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                      IF NOT G_isSalesCollection THEN
                    // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                        SalesLine.InitQtyToInvoice;
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" := 0;
                    SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice (Base)" := 0;
                  END;

                  IF (SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::Item) AND (SalesLine."No." <> '') THEN BEGIN
                    GetItem(SalesLine);
                    IF (Item."Costing Method" = Item."Costing Method"::Standard) AND NOT SalesLine.IsShipment THEN
                      SalesLine.GetUnitCost;
                  END;

                  IF SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" + SalesLine."Quantity Invoiced" <> SalesLine.Quantity THEN
                    EverythingInvoiced := FALSE;

                  IF SalesLine.Quantity = 0 THEN
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD(Amount,0)
                  ELSE BEGIN
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD("No.");
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD(Type);
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Gen. Bus. Posting Group");
                    SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Gen. Prod. Posting Group");
                    DivideAmount(1,SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice");
                  END;

                  IF SalesLine."Drop Shipment" THEN BEGIN
                    IF SalesLine.Type <> SalesLine.Type::Item THEN
                      SalesLine.TESTFIELD("Drop Shipment",FALSE);
                    IF (SalesLine."Qty. to Ship" <> 0) AND (SalesLine."Purch. Order Line No." = 0) THEN
                      ERROR(
                        Text009 +
                        Text010,
                        SalesLine."Line No.");
                  END;

                  RoundAmount(SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice");

                  IF NOT ("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::"Return Order","Document Type"::"Credit Memo"]) THEN BEGIN
                    ReverseAmount(SalesLine);
                    ReverseAmount(SalesLineACY);
                  END;

                  RemQtyToBeInvoiced := SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice";
                  RemQtyToBeInvoicedBase := SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice (Base)";

                  // Item Tracking:

                  // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                  IF (SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::Item) AND (SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
                    IF G_isWithEcoTax THEN BEGIN
                      SalesLine."Eco Tax Unit Amount" := G_CMCManagement.EcoTaxCalculationFromCU80(SalesLine,SalesHeader."Posting Date");
                      SalesLine."Eco Tax Unit Amount" := ROUND(SalesLine."Eco Tax Unit Amount",Currency."Unit-Amount Rounding Precision");
                      SalesLine."Eco Tax Total Amount" := SalesLine."Eco Tax Unit Amount" *  -SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice";
                      SalesLine."Eco Tax Total Amount" := ROUND(SalesLine."Eco Tax Total Amount" ,Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
                      TempSalesLine."Eco Tax Unit Amount" := SalesLine."Eco Tax Unit Amount";
                      TempSalesLine."Eco Tax Total Amount" := SalesLine."Eco Tax Total Amount";
                    END;
                  END;
                  // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04

                  // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                  IF NOT G_isSalesCollection THEN BEGIN
                  // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04

                    IF NOT SalesLine."Prepayment Line" THEN BEGIN
                      IF Invoice THEN
                        IF SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" = 0 THEN
                          TrackingSpecificationExists := FALSE
                        ELSE
                          TrackingSpecificationExists :=
                            ReserveSalesLine.RetrieveInvoiceSpecification(SalesLine,TempInvoicingSpecification);
                      EndLoop := FALSE;

                      IF "Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::"Return Order","Document Type"::"Credit Memo"] THEN BEGIN
                        IF ABS(RemQtyToBeInvoiced) > ABS(SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive") THEN BEGIN
                          ReturnRcptLine.RESET;
                          CASE "Document Type" OF
                            "Document Type"::"Return Order":
                              BEGIN
                                ReturnRcptLine.SETCURRENTKEY("Return Order No.","Return Order Line No.");
                                ReturnRcptLine.SETRANGE("Return Order No.",SalesLine."Document No.");
                                ReturnRcptLine.SETRANGE("Return Order Line No.",SalesLine."Line No.");
                              END;
                            "Document Type"::"Credit Memo":
                              BEGIN
                                ReturnRcptLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesLine."Return Receipt No.");
                                ReturnRcptLine.SETRANGE("Line No.",SalesLine."Return Receipt Line No.");
                              END;
                          END;
                          ReturnRcptLine.SETFILTER("Return Qty. Rcd. Not Invd.",'<>0');
                          IF ReturnRcptLine.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
                            ItemJnlRollRndg := TRUE;
                            REPEAT
                              IF TrackingSpecificationExists THEN BEGIN  // Item Tracking
                                ItemEntryRelation.GET(TempInvoicingSpecification."Appl.-to Item Entry");
                                ReturnRcptLine.GET(ItemEntryRelation."Source ID",ItemEntryRelation."Source Ref. No.");
                              END ELSE
                                ItemEntryRelation."Item Entry No." := ReturnRcptLine."Item Rcpt. Entry No.";
                              ReturnRcptLine.TESTFIELD("Sell-to Customer No.",SalesLine."Sell-to Customer No.");
                              ReturnRcptLine.TESTFIELD(Type,SalesLine.Type);
                              ReturnRcptLine.TESTFIELD("No.",SalesLine."No.");
                              ReturnRcptLine.TESTFIELD("Gen. Bus. Posting Group",SalesLine."Gen. Bus. Posting Group");
                              ReturnRcptLine.TESTFIELD("Gen. Prod. Posting Group",SalesLine."Gen. Prod. Posting Group");
                              ReturnRcptLine.TESTFIELD("Job No.",SalesLine."Job No.");
                              ReturnRcptLine.TESTFIELD("Unit of Measure Code",SalesLine."Unit of Measure Code");
                              ReturnRcptLine.TESTFIELD("Variant Code",SalesLine."Variant Code");
                              IF SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" * ReturnRcptLine.Quantity < 0 THEN
                                SalesLine.FIELDERROR("Qty. to Invoice",Text024);
                              IF TrackingSpecificationExists THEN BEGIN  // Item Tracking
                                QtyToBeInvoiced := TempInvoicingSpecification."Qty. to Invoice";
                                QtyToBeInvoicedBase := TempInvoicingSpecification."Qty. to Invoice (Base)";
                              END ELSE BEGIN
                                QtyToBeInvoiced := RemQtyToBeInvoiced - SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive";
                                QtyToBeInvoicedBase := RemQtyToBeInvoicedBase - SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive (Base)";
                              END;
                              IF ABS(QtyToBeInvoiced) >
                                 ABS(ReturnRcptLine.Quantity - ReturnRcptLine."Quantity Invoiced")
                              THEN BEGIN
                                QtyToBeInvoiced := ReturnRcptLine.Quantity - ReturnRcptLine."Quantity Invoiced";
                                QtyToBeInvoicedBase := ReturnRcptLine."Quantity (Base)" - ReturnRcptLine."Qty. Invoiced (Base)";
                              END;

                              IF TrackingSpecificationExists THEN
                                ItemTrackingMgt.AdjustQuantityRounding(
                                  RemQtyToBeInvoiced,QtyToBeInvoiced,
                                  RemQtyToBeInvoicedBase,QtyToBeInvoicedBase);

                              RemQtyToBeInvoiced := RemQtyToBeInvoiced - QtyToBeInvoiced;
                              RemQtyToBeInvoicedBase := RemQtyToBeInvoicedBase - QtyToBeInvoicedBase;
                              ReturnRcptLine."Quantity Invoiced" :=
                                ReturnRcptLine."Quantity Invoiced" + QtyToBeInvoiced;
                              ReturnRcptLine."Qty. Invoiced (Base)" :=
                                ReturnRcptLine."Qty. Invoiced (Base)" + QtyToBeInvoicedBase;
                              ReturnRcptLine."Return Qty. Rcd. Not Invd." :=
                                ReturnRcptLine.Quantity - ReturnRcptLine."Quantity Invoiced";

                              // --> 20100713-8061050-CMB6.00.01-FEC03
                              PosMgt_G.InsertSalesReturnInvEntry(ReturnRcptLine,SalesCrMemoHeader."No.",SalesLine."Line No.",
                                QtyToBeInvoiced,SalesCrMemoHeader."Posting Date");
                              // <-- 20100713-8061050-CMB6.00.01-FEC03

                              ReturnRcptLine.MODIFY;
                              IF SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::Item THEN
                                PostItemJnlLine(
                                  SalesLine,
                                  0,0,
                                  QtyToBeInvoiced,
                                  QtyToBeInvoicedBase,
                                  {ReturnRcptLine."Item Rcpt. Entry No."}
                                  ItemEntryRelation."Item Entry No.",'',TempInvoicingSpecification);
                              IF TrackingSpecificationExists THEN
                                EndLoop := (TempInvoicingSpecification.NEXT = 0)
                              ELSE
                                EndLoop :=
                                  (ReturnRcptLine.NEXT = 0) OR (ABS(RemQtyToBeInvoiced) <= ABS(SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive"));
                            UNTIL EndLoop;
                          END ELSE
                            ERROR(
                              Text025,
                              SalesLine."Return Receipt Line No.",SalesLine."Return Receipt No.");
                        END;

                        IF ABS(RemQtyToBeInvoiced) > ABS(SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive") THEN BEGIN
                          IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo" THEN
                            ERROR(
                              Text038,
                              ReturnRcptLine."Document No.");
                          ERROR(Text037);
                        END;

                      END ELSE BEGIN

                        IF ABS(RemQtyToBeInvoiced) > ABS(SalesLine."Qty. to Ship") THEN BEGIN
                          SalesShptLine.RESET;
                          CASE "Document Type" OF
                            "Document Type"::Order:
                              BEGIN
                                SalesShptLine.SETCURRENTKEY("Order No.","Order Line No.");
                                SalesShptLine.SETRANGE("Order No.",SalesLine."Document No.");
                                SalesShptLine.SETRANGE("Order Line No.",SalesLine."Line No.");
                              END;
                            "Document Type"::Invoice:
                              BEGIN
                                SalesShptLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesLine."Shipment No.");
                                SalesShptLine.SETRANGE("Line No.",SalesLine."Shipment Line No.");
                              END;
                          END;

                          SalesShptLine.SETFILTER("Qty. Shipped Not Invoiced",'<>0');
                          IF SalesShptLine.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
                            ItemJnlRollRndg := TRUE;
                            REPEAT
                              IF TrackingSpecificationExists THEN BEGIN
                                ItemEntryRelation.GET(TempInvoicingSpecification."Appl.-to Item Entry");
                                SalesShptLine.GET(ItemEntryRelation."Source ID",ItemEntryRelation."Source Ref. No.");
                              END ELSE
                                ItemEntryRelation."Item Entry No." := SalesShptLine."Item Shpt. Entry No.";
                              SalesShptLine.TESTFIELD("Sell-to Customer No.",SalesLine."Sell-to Customer No.");
                              SalesShptLine.TESTFIELD(Type,SalesLine.Type);
                              SalesShptLine.TESTFIELD("No.",SalesLine."No.");
                              SalesShptLine.TESTFIELD("Gen. Bus. Posting Group",SalesLine."Gen. Bus. Posting Group");
                              SalesShptLine.TESTFIELD("Gen. Prod. Posting Group",SalesLine."Gen. Prod. Posting Group");
                              SalesShptLine.TESTFIELD("Job No.",SalesLine."Job No.");
                              SalesShptLine.TESTFIELD("Unit of Measure Code",SalesLine."Unit of Measure Code");
                              SalesShptLine.TESTFIELD("Variant Code",SalesLine."Variant Code");
                              IF (-SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice") * SalesShptLine.Quantity < 0 THEN
                                SalesLine.FIELDERROR("Qty. to Invoice",Text011);
                              IF TrackingSpecificationExists THEN BEGIN
                                QtyToBeInvoiced := TempInvoicingSpecification."Qty. to Invoice";
                                QtyToBeInvoicedBase := TempInvoicingSpecification."Qty. to Invoice (Base)";
                              END ELSE BEGIN
                                QtyToBeInvoiced := RemQtyToBeInvoiced - SalesLine."Qty. to Ship";
                                QtyToBeInvoicedBase := RemQtyToBeInvoicedBase - SalesLine."Qty. to Ship (Base)";
                              END;
                              IF ABS(QtyToBeInvoiced) >
                                 ABS(SalesShptLine.Quantity - SalesShptLine."Quantity Invoiced")
                              THEN BEGIN
                                QtyToBeInvoiced := -(SalesShptLine.Quantity - SalesShptLine."Quantity Invoiced");
                                QtyToBeInvoicedBase := -(SalesShptLine."Quantity (Base)" - SalesShptLine."Qty. Invoiced (Base)");
                              END;

                              IF TrackingSpecificationExists THEN
                                ItemTrackingMgt.AdjustQuantityRounding(
                                  RemQtyToBeInvoiced,QtyToBeInvoiced,
                                  RemQtyToBeInvoicedBase,QtyToBeInvoicedBase);

                              RemQtyToBeInvoiced := RemQtyToBeInvoiced - QtyToBeInvoiced;
                              RemQtyToBeInvoicedBase := RemQtyToBeInvoicedBase - QtyToBeInvoicedBase;
                              SalesShptLine."Quantity Invoiced" :=
                                SalesShptLine."Quantity Invoiced" - QtyToBeInvoiced;
                              SalesShptLine."Qty. Invoiced (Base)" :=
                                SalesShptLine."Qty. Invoiced (Base)" - QtyToBeInvoicedBase;
                              SalesShptLine."Qty. Shipped Not Invoiced" :=
                                SalesShptLine.Quantity - SalesShptLine."Quantity Invoiced";
                              ShipmentInvoiced.INIT;
                              ShipmentInvoiced."Invoice No." := SalesInvHeader."No.";
                              ShipmentInvoiced."Shipment No." := SalesShptLine."Document No.";
                              ShipmentInvoiced."Shipment Line No." := SalesShptLine."Line No.";
                              ShipmentInvoiced."Invoice Line No." := SalesLine."Line No.";
                              ShipmentInvoiced."Qty. to Invoice" := -QtyToBeInvoiced;
                              ShipmentInvoiced."Qty. to Ship" := SalesShptLine.Quantity;
                              ShipmentInvoiced."Posting Date" := SalesInvHeader."Posting Date";
                              ShipmentInvoiced."User ID" := USERID;
                              IF ShipmentInvoiced.INSERT THEN;
                              // --> 20100713-8061050-CMB6.00.01-FEC01
                              PosMgt_G.InsertSalesShipInvEntry(SalesShptLine,SalesInvHeader."No.",SalesLine."Line No.",
                                -QtyToBeInvoiced,SalesInvHeader."Posting Date");
                              // <-- 20100713-8061050-CMB6.00.01-FEC01
                              SalesShptLine.MODIFY;
                              IF SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::Item THEN
                                PostItemJnlLine(
                                  SalesLine,
                                  0,0,
                                  QtyToBeInvoiced,
                                  QtyToBeInvoicedBase,
                                  {SalesShptLine."Item Shpt. Entry No."}
                                  ItemEntryRelation."Item Entry No.",'',TempInvoicingSpecification);
                              IF TrackingSpecificationExists THEN
                                EndLoop := (TempInvoicingSpecification.NEXT = 0)
                              ELSE
                                EndLoop :=
                                  (SalesShptLine.NEXT = 0) OR (ABS(RemQtyToBeInvoiced) <= ABS(SalesLine."Qty. to Ship"))
                            UNTIL EndLoop;
                          END ELSE
                            ERROR(
                              Text026,
                              SalesLine."Shipment Line No.",SalesLine."Shipment No.");
                        END;

                        IF ABS(RemQtyToBeInvoiced) > ABS(SalesLine."Qty. to Ship") THEN BEGIN
                          IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::Invoice THEN
                            ERROR(
                              Text027,
                              SalesShptLine."Document No.");
                          ERROR(Text013);
                        END;
                      END;

                      IF TrackingSpecificationExists THEN
                        SaveInvoiceSpecification(TempInvoicingSpecification);
                    END;

                  // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                  // cas de la collecte
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    QtyToBeInvoiced := SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice";
                    QtyToBeInvoicedBase := SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice (Base)";
                    RemQtyToBeInvoiced := 0;
                    RemQtyToBeInvoicedBase := 0;

                    IF SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::Item THEN BEGIN
                      // --> 20140128-Cer17-CMC02.02.00-1333-SDE01
                      IF SalesLine."Posted Delivery No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
                      // <-- 20140128-Cer17-CMC02.02.00-1333-SDE01

                        // --> 20140918-CER46-TER01.01.00-PSC01
                        // BEFORE
                        //L_recPostedDelivery.GET(L_recPostedDelivery."Delivery Type"::Delivery,SalesLine."Posted Delivery No.");
                        //AFTER
                        IF NOT L_recPostedDelivery.GET(L_recPostedDelivery."Delivery Type"::Delivery,SalesLine."Posted Delivery No.") THEN
                          L_recPostedDelivery.GET(L_recPostedDelivery."Delivery Type"::Resumption,SalesLine."Posted Delivery No.");
                        // 20180828-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-ID36617-JLT01 -->
                        IF L_recPostedDelivery.Canceled=TRUE THEN ERROR(Text60010,L_recPostedDelivery."No.",SalesLine."Document No.");
                        //<-- 20180828-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-ID36617-JLT01
                        // <-- 20140918-CER46-TER01.01.00-PSC01
                        ItemLedgShptEntryNo := L_recPostedDelivery."Item Ledger Entry No.";
                        // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
                        IF L_recPostedDelivery."On Behalf Of" THEN
                          SetIcCollectPost(TRUE);
                        // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
                        PostItemJnlLine(SalesLine,0,0,QtyToBeInvoiced,QtyToBeInvoicedBase,
                          ItemLedgShptEntryNo,'',TempInvoicingSpecification);
                        // --> 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
                        SetIcCollectPost(FALSE);
                        // <-- 20160513-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2813-NED
                        L_recPostedDelivery."Unit Price Invoiced" :=  SalesLine."Unit Price";
                        L_recPostedDelivery.Invoiced := TRUE;
                        L_recPostedDelivery."Sales Document No." := '';

                        CASE "Document Type" OF
                          "Document Type"::Invoice :
                            BEGIN
                              L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Sales Document Type" :=
                                L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Sales Document Type"::"Posted invoice";
                              L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Sales Document No" := SalesInvHeader."No.";
                            END;
                          "Document Type"::"Credit Memo":
                            BEGIN
                              L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Sales Document Type" :=
                                L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Sales Document Type"::"Posted Credit Memo";
                              L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Sales Document No" := SalesCrMemoHeader."No.";
                            END;

                        END;

                        L_recPostedDelivery.MODIFY;

                        // --> 20140918-CER46-TER01.01.00-PSC01
                        IF (L_recPostedDelivery."Delivery Type" = L_recPostedDelivery."Delivery Type"::Delivery) AND
                           (L_recPostedDelivery."Related Posted Delivery No." <> '')
                        THEN BEGIN
                          IF CMCPostedReturn_L.GET(
                            CMCPostedReturn_L."Delivery Type"::Resumption,L_recPostedDelivery."Related Posted Delivery No.")
                          THEN BEGIN
                            CMCPostedReturn_L."Unit Price Invoiced" := L_recPostedDelivery."Unit Price Invoiced";
                            CMCPostedReturn_L.Invoiced := L_recPostedDelivery.Invoiced;
                            CMCPostedReturn_L."Sales Document No." := L_recPostedDelivery."Sales Document No.";
                            CMCPostedReturn_L."Posted Sales Document Type" := L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Sales Document Type";
                            CMCPostedReturn_L."Posted Sales Document No" := L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Sales Document No";
                            CMCPostedReturn_L.MODIFY;
                          END;
                        END;
                        // <-- 20140918-CER46-TER01.01.00-PSC01

                      // --> 20140712-Cer17-CMC02.02.00-1333-SDE01
                      END ELSE BEGIN
                        // cas des reprises factur'es
                        IF (SalesLine."Collection Recept No."<>'') AND
                           (RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L.GET(RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Receipt Type"::Resumption,
                           SalesLine."Collection Recept No.",SalesLine."Collection Recept Line"))
                        THEN BEGIN
                          PostedReceipt_L.GET(RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Receipt Type",RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."No.");
                          // cas de la vente totale de la reprise
                          // --> 20150624-CER41-TER01.01.00-383-SDE01
                          //IF SalesLine."Collection Recept Line"= 10000 THEN BEGIN
                          IF (SalesLine."Collection Recept Line"= 10000) OR
                            (RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Collection Type"= RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Collection Type"::Sales)
                          THEN BEGIN
                          // <-- 20150624-CER41-TER01.01.00-383-SDE01
                            // --> 20151125-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2482-FBE01
                            IF PostedReceipt_L."On Behalf Of" THEN
                              SetIcCollectPost(TRUE);
                            // <-- 20151125-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2482-FBE01
                            ItemLedgShptEntryNo := RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Item Ledger Entry No.";
                            PostItemJnlLine(SalesLine,0,0,QtyToBeInvoiced,QtyToBeInvoicedBase,
                              ItemLedgShptEntryNo,'',TempInvoicingSpecification);
                            // --> 20151125-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2482-FBE01
                            IF PostedReceipt_L."On Behalf Of" THEN
                              SetIcCollectPost(FALSE);
                            // <-- 20151125-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2482-FBE01
                            CASE "Document Type" OF
                              "Document Type"::Invoice:
                                BEGIN
                                  RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document Type" :=
                                    RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document Type"::"Posted invoice";
                                  RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document No" := SalesInvHeader."No.";
                                END;
                              // --> 20160526-GEN04-CMC02.02.01-2905-NED01
                              "Document Type"::"Credit Memo":
                                BEGIN
                                  RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document Type" :=
                                    RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document Type"::"Posted Credit Memo";
                                  RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document No" := SalesCrMemoHeader."No.";
                                END;
                              // <-- 20160526-GEN04-CMC02.02.01-2905-NED01
                            END;
                            RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L.Invoiced:=TRUE;
                            RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L.MODIFY;
                          END ELSE BEGIN
                            // cas de la vente partielle de la reprise
                            // --> 20160526-GEN04-CMC02.02.01-2905-NED01
                            //RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L.GET(RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L."Receipt Type",
                            RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L.GET(RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Receipt Type",
                            // <-- 20160526-GEN04-CMC02.02.01-2905-NED01
                            RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."No.", 10000);
                            // --> 20150902-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2186-FBE01
                            IF PostedReceipt_L."On Behalf Of" THEN
                              SetIcCollectPost(TRUE);
                            // <-- 20150902-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2186-FBE01
                            ItemLedgShptEntryNo := RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Item Ledger Entry No.";
                              PostItemJnlLine(
                                SalesLine,
                                0,0,
                                QtyToBeInvoiced,QtyToBeInvoicedBase,
                                ItemLedgShptEntryNo,'',TempInvoicingSpecification);
                            // --> 20150902-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2186-FBE01
                            IF PostedReceipt_L."On Behalf Of" THEN
                              SetIcCollectPost(FALSE);
                            // <-- 20150902-GEN15/001-ADV01.01.00-2186-FBE01
                              CASE "Document Type" OF
                                "Document Type"::Invoice:
                                  BEGIN
                                    RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document Type" :=
                                      RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document Type"::"Posted invoice";
                                    RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document No" := SalesInvHeader."No.";

                                    RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L."Posted Sales Document Type" :=
                                      RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L."Posted Sales Document Type"::"Posted invoice";
                                    RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L."Posted Sales Document No" := SalesInvHeader."No.";
                                  END;
                                // --> 20160526-GEN04-CMC02.02.01-2905-NED01
                                "Document Type"::"Credit Memo":
                                  BEGIN
                                    RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document Type" :=
                                      RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document Type"::"Posted Credit Memo";
                                    RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L."Posted Sales Document No" := SalesCrMemoHeader."No.";

                                    RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L."Posted Sales Document Type" :=
                                      RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L."Posted Sales Document Type"::"Posted Credit Memo";
                                    RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L."Posted Sales Document No" := SalesCrMemoHeader."No.";
                                  END;
                                // <-- 20160526-GEN04-CMC02.02.01-2905-NED01
                              END;
                              RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L.Invoiced:=TRUE;
                              RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine_L.MODIFY;
                              RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L.Invoiced:=TRUE;
                              RecCMCPostedReceiptsLine2_L.MODIFY;
                          END;
                        END;
                      END;
                      // <-- 20140712-Cer17-CMC02.02.00-1333-SDE01
                    END;
                    // --> 20140926-CER50-TER01.01.00-SCF01
                    //IF (("Invoice Type"="Invoice Type"::"PrePay Oilseeds") AND
                    //    (SalesLine.Type = SalesLine.Type::"G/L Account"))
                    IF ("Invoice Type"="Invoice Type"::"PrePay Oilseeds")
                    // <-- 20140926-CER50-TER01.01.00-SCF01
                    THEN BEGIN
                      // --> 20160601-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2796-MMI01
                      {BEFORE
                      IF L_recPostedDelivery.GET(L_recPostedDelivery."Delivery Type"::Delivery,SalesLine."Posted Delivery No.") THEN BEGIN
                      AFTER}
                      L_recPostedDelivery.RESET;
                      L_recPostedDelivery.SETFILTER("Delivery Type", '%1|%2', L_recPostedDelivery."Delivery Type"::Delivery,
                        L_recPostedDelivery."Delivery Type"::Resumption);
                      L_recPostedDelivery.SETRANGE("No.", SalesLine."Posted Delivery No.");
                      IF L_recPostedDelivery.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                      // <-- 20160601-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2796-MMI01
                        L_recPostedDelivery."Invoiced Pre-payment" := TRUE;
                        // --> 20151007-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2162-FHS01
                        IF L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Prepay Invoice No." = '' THEN BEGIN
                          IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::Invoice THEN BEGIN
                            L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Prepay Invoice Type" :=
                              L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Prepay Invoice Type"::"Posted invoice";
                        // <-- 20151007-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2162-FHS01
                        // --> 20150803-WI00-TER01.01.00-1773-PDC01
                        // --> 20150326-CER97-TER01.01.00-059-RMZ01
                        //L_recPostedDelivery."Prepay Invoice No." := SalesInvHeader."No.";
                        L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Prepay Invoice No." := SalesInvHeader."No.";
                            // --> 20190401-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3401-MRU01
                            L_recPostedDelivery."Prepay Invoice No." := '';
                            // <-- 20190401-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3401-MRU01
                        // <-- 20150326-CER97-TER01.01.00-059-RMZ01
                        // <-- 20150803-WI00-TER01.01.00-1773-PDC01
                        // --> 20151007-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2162-FHS01
                          END;
                          IF "Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo" THEN BEGIN
                            L_recPostedDelivery."Posted Prepay Invoice Type" :=

              IF NOT ("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::"Return Order","Document Type"::"Credit Memo"]) THEN BEGIN

                // <-- 20101213-8060990/003-CMB6.00.01-JDL03

                // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
                GenJnlLine."Activity Code":= GetActivityCode(SalesHeader);
                // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04

                TempJnlLineDim.DELETEALL;
                TempDocDim.RESET;
                TempDocDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Sales Header");
                DimMgt.CopyDocDimToJnlLineDim(TempDocDim,TempJnlLineDim);
                GenJnlPostLine.RunWithCheck(GenJnlLine,TempJnlLineDim);

                // --> 20170119-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01
                // --> 20200708-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3485-SCR01
                { BEFORE
                IF SalesSetup."Prepayment Cust Posting Group" <> '' THEN
                  AFTER }

                IF SalesSetup."Gestion avance appro" THEN
                // <-- 20200708-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3485-SCR01
                // <-- 20170119-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01
                // --> 20170116-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01
                // Ajouter toutes les 'critures de transfert de collectif pour l'avance APPRO
                IF SalesHeader."Document Type" = SalesHeader."Document Type":: Invoice THEN BEGIN
                  // --> 20180105-WI00-ADV01.01.00-32002-JCD01
                  {
                  // --> 20171223-WI00-ADV01.01.00-32002-JCD01
                  PrepSalesDisc_L.LOCKTABLE;
                  // <-- 20171223-WI00-ADV01.01.00-32002-JCD01
                  }
                  // <-- 20180105-WI00-ADV01.01.00-32002-JCD01

                  PrepSalesDisc_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.",SalesHeader."Bill-to Customer No.");

                  PrepSalesDisc_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesHeader."No.");

                    AFTER }

                    PrepSalesDisc_L.CALCSUMS("Include VAT Apply Amount");
                      // --> 20170119-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01

                      DiffMax_L := SalesSetup."Prep. Max. Difference Allowed";
                      DecDisc_L := PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount" + TotalSalesLine."Amount Including VAT";

                      IF ABS(DecDisc_L) >= DiffMax_L THEN BEGIN
                        // La facture solde le montant acompte
                        // V'rification que le montant ne d'passe pas le montant restant en avance appro
                        // Si petit 'cart : solde le compte APPRO (on charge Desdisc_L du montant pour solder le compte)
                        DecDisc_L := 0;
                        // --> 20200708-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3485-SCR01
                        //(Sup) CustPostingGroup_L.GET(SalesSetup."Prepayment Cust Posting Group");
                        // <-- 20200708-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3485-SCR01
                        CustLedgerEntry_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Customer No.",Open,"GLAccountNo.","Posting Date");
                        CustLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.","Bill-to Customer No.");
                        CustLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE(Open,TRUE);
                        CustLedgerEntry_L.SETRANGE("GLAccountNo.",CustPostingGroup_L."Receivables Account");
                        DecAmountOpen_L := 0;
                        IF CustLedgerEntry_L.FIND('-') THEN REPEAT
                          CustLedgerEntry_L.CALCFIELDS("Remaining Amount");
                          DecAmountOpen_L += CustLedgerEntry_L."Remaining Amount";
                        UNTIL CustLedgerEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
                        IF (PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount" + DecAmountOpen_L) <> 0 THEN
                          IF ABS(PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount" + DecAmountOpen_L) < DiffMax_L THEN
                            DecDisc_L := DecAmountOpen_L + PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount";
                      END;
                      IF (DecDisc_L <> 0) AND (ABS(DecDisc_L) < DiffMax_L) THEN BEGIN
                        // Equilibre pour que le montant soit 'gale au montant de la facture ou que le montant solde le compte avance appro
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L.INIT;
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L."Entry No." := 0;
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L.Type := PrepSalesDisc2_L.Type::Discount;
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L."Document No." := "No.";
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L."Line Document No." := 0;

                        PrepSalesDisc2_L."Customer No." := "Bill-to Customer No.";
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L.Description := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text60001,SalesHeader."Bill-to Name"),1,50);
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L."Due Date" := "Due Date";
                        // --> 20170123-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L."Ref Entry No." := 99999999;
                        // <-- 20170123-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L.VALIDATE(Amount,-DecDisc_L);
                        // --> 20200708-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3485-SCR01
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L."Prepayment Cust Posting Group" := CustPostGroup_L;
                        // <-- 20200708-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3485-SCR01
                        PrepSalesDisc2_L.INSERT;
                        PrepSalesDisc_L.CALCSUMS("Include VAT Apply Amount");
                      END;
                      // <-- 20170119-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01
                    IF PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount" <> 0 THEN
                      FOR iDisc_L := 1 TO 2 DO BEGIN
                        GenJnlLine.INIT;
                        GenJnlLine."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";

                        // --> 20170125-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01
                        {BEFORE
                        GenJnlLine.Description := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text60001,SalesHeader."Bill-to Name"),1,50);
                        AFTER}
                        // --> 20230124-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3618-CJY01
                        {BEFORE
                        GenJnlLine.Description := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text60002,GenJnlLineDocNo),1,50);
                        AFTER}
                        //on d'termine notre libell' en fonction du type d'avance que l'on doit retrouver de maniSre unique dans les remises
                        // on consitute le libell' par d'fatu au cas ou on ne trouverait pas de param'trage ci-dessous
                        LabelPostingPrep_L := Text60031 + ' ' + STRSUBSTNO(Text60030,GenJnlLineDocNo);

                          CMCSalesDiscount_L.SETRANGE("Prepayment Cust Posting Group",CustPostGroup_L);

                            IF CMCSalesDiscount_L."Label ledger entry post. prep" <> '' THEN
                              LabelPostingPrep_L := COPYSTR(CMCSalesDiscount_L."Label ledger entry post. prep" + ' ' +
                                STRSUBSTNO(Text60030,GenJnlLineDocNo),1,50);
                            //ELSE
                          END
                        END;

                        //GenJnlLine.Description := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(Text60002,GenJnlLineDocNo),1,50);

                        // <-- 20170125-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01
                        GenJnlLine."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" := "Shortcut Dimension 1 Code";
                        GenJnlLine."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" := "Shortcut Dimension 2 Code";
                        GenJnlLine."Reason Code" := "Reason Code";
                        GenJnlLine."Account Type" := GenJnlLine."Account Type"::Customer;
                        GenJnlLine."Account No." := "Bill-to Customer No.";
                        GenJnlLine."Third Grouping" := "Third Grouping";
                        GenJnlLine."Document Type" := GenJnlLine."Document Type"::" ";
                        GenJnlLine."Document No." := GenJnlLineDocNo;
                        GenJnlLine."External Document No." := GenJnlLineExtDocNo;
                        GenJnlLine."Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
                        IF SalesHeader."Currency Code" = '' THEN
                          GenJnlLine."Currency Factor" := 1
                        ELSE
                          GenJnlLine."Currency Factor" :=  SalesHeader."Currency Factor";
                        IF iDisc_L = 1 THEN BEGIN
                          // Annulation 'criture de facture
                          // --> 20190121-WI00-ADV01.01.00-ID40031-MRU01
                          //GenJnlLine.Amount := - PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount";
                          // <-- 20190121-WI00-ADV01.01.00-ID40031-MRU01
                          GenJnlLine."Source Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
                          GenJnlLine."Source Currency Amount" := - PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount";
                          //-TotalSalesLine."Amount Including VAT";
                          GenJnlLine."Amount (LCY)" := -PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount";
                          GenJnlLine."Posting Group" := "Customer Posting Group";
                          GenJnlLine."Document Type" := GenJnlLine."Document Type"::Payment;
                            // --> 20170119-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01
                            IF ABS(TotalSalesLine."Amount Including VAT") = PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount" THEN BEGIN
                              GenJnlLine."Applies-to Doc. Type" := GenJnlLine."Applies-to Doc. Type"::Invoice;
                              GenJnlLine.VALIDATE("Applies-to Doc. No.",GenJnlLineDocNo);
                            END;
                            // <-- 20170119-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3187-JCD01
                          // --> 20190121-WI00-ADV01.01.00-ID40031-MRU01
                          // --> 20240405-WI00-ADV01.01.00-CC76108-MRU01
                          //GenJnlLine.Amount := - PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount";
                          GenJnlLine.Amount := - ROUND(PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount", Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
                          // <-- 20240405-WI00-ADV01.01.00-CC76108-MRU01
                          // <-- 20190121-WI00-ADV01.01.00-ID40031-MRU01
                        END ELSE BEGIN
                          // Prise sur le compte Avance Appro
                          // --> 20240405-WI00-ADV01.01.00-CC76108-MRU01
                          //GenJnlLine.Amount := PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount";//-TotalSalesLine."Amount Including VAT";
                          GenJnlLine.Amount := ROUND(PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount", Currency."Amount Rounding Precision");
                          // <-- 20240405-WI00-ADV01.01.00-CC76108-MRU01
                          GenJnlLine."Source Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
                          GenJnlLine."Source Currency Amount" := PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount";
                          GenJnlLine."Amount (LCY)" := PrepSalesDisc_L."Include VAT Apply Amount";
                          // --> 20200708-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3485-SCR01
                          { BEFORE
                          GenJnlLine."Posting Group" := SalesSetup."Prepayment Cust Posting Group";
                            AFTER }
                          GenJnlLine."Posting Group" := CustPostGroup_L;
                          // <-- 20200708-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3485-SCR01
                        END;
                        IF GenJnlLine."Currency Factor" <> 1 THEN BEGIN
                          GenJnlLine."Amount (LCY)" :=

                        GenJnlLine.Correction := Correction;
                        GenJnlLine."Sell-to/Buy-from No." := "Sell-to Customer No.";
                        GenJnlLine."Bill-to/Pay-to No." := "Bill-to Customer No.";

                        GenJnlLine."System-Created Entry" := TRUE;
                        IF DueDate_G = 0D THEN
                          GenJnlLine."Due Date" := "Due Date"
                        ELSE

                        GenJnlLine."Payment Terms Code" := "Payment Terms Code";
                        GenJnlLine."Source Type" := GenJnlLine."Source Type"::Customer;

                        GenJnlLine."Sell-to Contact No." := "Sell-to Contact No.";

                    // Transfert des 'critures de remise appro vers remise appro enregistr'e
                    IF PrepSalesDisc_L.FIND('-') THEN REPEAT

                      PostedPrepSalesDisc_L."Invoice No." := GenJnlLineDocNo;

                      PrepSalesDisc_L.DELETE;
                    UNTIL PrepSalesDisc_L.NEXT = 0;

                    // Suppression des 'critures de Type enregistr'es si plus de facture en cours
                    PrepSalesDisc_L.SETFILTER("Document No.",'<>%1',SalesHeader."No.");

                      PrepSalesDisc_L.SETRANGE("Document No.");
                      PrepSalesDisc_L.SETRANGE(Type);
                      PrepSalesDisc_L.DELETEALL;
                    END;
                  END;
                END;

                // --> 20240115-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3643-IJI01
                IF SalesHeader."Document Type" = SalesHeader."Document Type"::Invoice THEN BEGIN
                  NegativeInvDiscLine_L.SETRANGE("Invoice No.",Rec."No.");

                    NegativeInvDiscLine_L.MODIFYALL("Posted Invoice No.",GenJnlLineDocNo);

                IF SalesHeader."Document Type" = SalesHeader."Document Type"::"Credit Memo" THEN BEGIN

                  IF NegativeInvDiscLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN

                END;

                PrepMgt_G.FillInPrepLogEntry(SalesHeader,GenJnlLineDocNo,"Posting Date");

                IF SalesHeader."Document Type" = SalesHeader."Document Type"::"Credit Memo" THEN
                  PrepMgt_G.FillPostedDocNo(SalesHeader,GenJnlLineDocNo);

                // Balancing account
                IF "Bal. Account No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  IF ShowDialog THEN  //LS
                    Window.UPDATE(5,1);
                  IF NOT IsOnlinePayment(SalesHeader) THEN
                    PostBalancing(TempJnlLineDim,0)
                END;
              END;

              IF ICGenJnlLineNo > 0 THEN
                PostICGenJnl;

              InvtSetup.GET;
              IF InvtSetup."Automatic Cost Adjustment" <>
                 InvtSetup."Automatic Cost Adjustment"::Never
              THEN BEGIN
                InvtAdjmt.SetProperties(TRUE,InvtSetup."Automatic Cost Posting");
                InvtAdjmt.MakeMultiLevelAdjmt;
              END;
              // --> 20130911-App29-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
              IF "Automatic Set off" THEN
                AutoSetOff(SalesHeader);
              // <-- 20130911-App29-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
              // Modify/delete sales header and sales lines
              IF NOT RECORDLEVELLOCKING THEN BEGIN
                IF WhseReceive THEN
                  WhseRcptLine.LOCKTABLE(TRUE,TRUE);
                IF WhseShip THEN
                  WhseShptLine.LOCKTABLE(TRUE,TRUE);

                "Last Posting No." := "Posting No.";
                "Posting No." := '';
              END;

                "Last Return Receipt No." := "Return Receipt No.";

              // --> 20110207-8061050-CMB6.00.10-FEC01
              GLSetup.GET;
              IF (GLSetup."Commitment on Sales Order" AND ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::Order)) OR
                 (GLSetup."Commitment on Sales Return" AND ("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Return Order"))
              THEN
                EverythingInvoiced := FALSE;
              // <-- 20110207-8061050-CMB6.00.10-FEC01

              IF ("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::Order,"Document Type"::"Return Order"]) AND
                 (NOT EverythingInvoiced)
              THEN BEGIN
                MODIFY;
                // Insert T336 records

                IF SalesLine.FINDSET THEN
                  REPEAT
                    IF SalesLine.Quantity <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                      IF Ship THEN BEGIN

                          SalesLine."Quantity Shipped" +

                          SalesLine."Qty. Shipped (Base)" +

                        SalesLine."Driver Name" := '';
                        SalesLine."Full Lorry" := FALSE;
                        // <-- 20140506-APP27-CMC02.02.00-2087-FEC01

                        // --> 20140918-GEN14-CMC02.02.00-1685-FBE01
                        //IF SalesLine."Quantity before delivery" = 0 THEN

                        SalesLine."Actual Shipping Agent Code" := '';
                        SalesLine.Consignment := '';
                        SalesLine."Truck Registration" := '';
                        SalesLine."Driver Name" := '';
                        SalesLine."Full Lorry" := FALSE;
                        // <-- 20140529-8060990/101-CMB6.00.10.09-2168-FEC01

                        // --> 20120112-Int01-CMC02.01.00-DRN01
                        cduWeighingManagement_L.InitWeighingDataToSalesLine(SalesLine);
                        // <-- 20120112-Int01-CMC02.01.00-DRN01

                        // --> 20140513-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2107-FEC02
                        IF Receive AND (SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive" <> 0) AND SalesLine."Update Source Order" THEN
