    PROCEDURE EcoTaxCalculation@8053001(P_recSalesLine@8053000 : Record 37) RES_EcoTaxeUnitAmount : Decimal;
    VAR
      L_RecItem@8053001 : Record 27;
      L_RecItemUnit@8053002 : Record 5404;
      L_SalesQtyPerUnit@8053003 : Decimal;
      L_AMMQtyPerUnit@8053004 : Decimal;
      L_BaseQtyPerUnit@8053005 : Decimal;
      L_RecCMCItemActiveSubst@8053006 : Record 8053024;
      L_RecCMCActiveSubstCatRate@8053007 : Record 8053022;
      L_RecCMCActiveSubstance@8053008 : Record 8053023;
      L_TaxPerKg@8053009 : Decimal;
      L_TaxInBaseUnit@8053010 : Decimal;
      L_TaxInSalesUnit@8053011 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
      RES_EcoTaxeUnitAmount := 0;

      L_SalesQtyPerUnit := 0;
      L_AMMQtyPerUnit := 0;
      L_TaxPerKg := 0;

      L_RecItemUnit.RESET;
      L_RecCMCItemActiveSubst.RESET;
      L_RecCMCActiveSubstCatRate.RESET;

      L_SalesQtyPerUnit := P_recSalesLine."Qty. per Unit of Measure"; // Quantit' par unit' de vente

      IF P_recSalesLine.Type = P_recSalesLine.Type::Item THEN BEGIN
        L_RecItem.GET(P_recSalesLine."No.");
        IF L_RecItem."Unit AMM Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          L_RecItemUnit.GET(P_recSalesLine."No.",L_RecItem."Unit AMM Code");
          L_AMMQtyPerUnit := L_RecItemUnit."Qty. per Unit of Measure"; // Quantit' par unit' AMM

          L_RecCMCItemActiveSubst.SETCURRENTKEY("Item No.");
          L_RecCMCItemActiveSubst.SETRANGE("Item No.",P_recSalesLine."No.");
          IF L_RecCMCItemActiveSubst.FINDSET THEN
            REPEAT
              L_RecCMCActiveSubstance.GET(L_RecCMCItemActiveSubst."CAS Code");
              L_RecCMCActiveSubstCatRate.SETCURRENTKEY("Starting Date");
              L_RecCMCActiveSubstCatRate.SETRANGE(Code,L_RecCMCActiveSubstance."Category Code");
              L_RecCMCActiveSubstCatRate.SETFILTER("Starting Date",'<=%1',WORKDATE);
              IF L_RecCMCActiveSubstCatRate.FINDLAST THEN
                L_TaxPerKg := L_RecCMCActiveSubstCatRate."Tax/ KG" * L_RecCMCItemActiveSubst.Quantity;

              // Taxe par quantit' de substance active
              // ----------------------------------------------------------------------
              L_TaxInBaseUnit := L_TaxPerKg/L_AMMQtyPerUnit; // Taxe par unit' de base
              L_TaxInSalesUnit := L_TaxInBaseUnit * L_SalesQtyPerUnit; // Taxe par unit' de vente

              RES_EcoTaxeUnitAmount := RES_EcoTaxeUnitAmount + L_TaxInSalesUnit;

            UNTIL L_RecCMCItemActiveSubst.NEXT = 0
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(ROUND(RES_EcoTaxeUnitAmount));

      // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE FillInvPostingBufferForEcoTax@8053002(P_recSalesLine@1000 : Record 37;P_recSalesLineACY@1001 : Record 37);
    BEGIN
      // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04

      // Copie et Adaptation de la fonction Standard FillInvPostingBuffer

      WITH P_recSalesLine DO BEGIN

        IF ("Gen. Bus. Posting Group" <> GenPostingSetup."Gen. Bus. Posting Group") OR
           ("Gen. Prod. Posting Group" <> GenPostingSetup."Gen. Prod. Posting Group")
        THEN
          GenPostingSetup.GET("Gen. Bus. Posting Group","Gen. Prod. Posting Group");

        CLEAR(InvPostingBuffer[1]);

        InvPostingBuffer[1].Type := Type;
        // --> 20180119-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3322-JCD01
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."CMC Campaign No." := "CMC Campaign No.";
        IF Type = Type::Item THEN
          InvPostingBuffer[1].Quantity := "Qty. to Invoice (Base)";
        // <-- 20180119-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3322-JCD01

        IF (Type = Type::"G/L Account") OR (Type = Type::"Fixed Asset") THEN
          InvPostingBuffer[1]."G/L Account" := "No."
        ELSE
          IF "Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::"Return Order","Document Type"::"Credit Memo"] THEN BEGIN
            GenPostingSetup.TESTFIELD("Eco Taxe Account No.");
            InvPostingBuffer[1]."G/L Account" := GenPostingSetup."Eco Taxe Account No.";
          END ELSE BEGIN
             GenPostingSetup.TESTFIELD("Eco Taxe Account No.");
             InvPostingBuffer[1]."G/L Account" := GenPostingSetup."Eco Taxe Account No.";
          END;

        InvPostingBuffer[1]."System-Created Entry" := TRUE;
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."Gen. Bus. Posting Group" := "Gen. Bus. Posting Group";
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."Gen. Prod. Posting Group" := "Gen. Prod. Posting Group";
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."VAT Bus. Posting Group" := "VAT Bus. Posting Group";
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."VAT Prod. Posting Group" := "VAT Prod. Posting Group";
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."VAT Calculation Type" := "VAT Calculation Type";
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."Global Dimension 1 Code" := "Shortcut Dimension 1 Code";
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."Global Dimension 2 Code" := "Shortcut Dimension 2 Code";
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."Job No." := "Job No.";
        InvPostingBuffer[1].Amount := -"Eco Tax Total Amount" ;
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."VAT Base Amount" := 0;
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."Amount (ACY)" := -"Eco Tax Total Amount";
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."VAT Base Amount (ACY)" := 0;
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."VAT Amount" := 0;
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."VAT Amount (ACY)" := 0;
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."VAT Difference" := 0;
        InvPostingBuffer[1]."VAT %" := 0;

        IF Type = Type::"Fixed Asset" THEN BEGIN
          InvPostingBuffer[1]."FA Posting Date" := "FA Posting Date";
          InvPostingBuffer[1]."Depreciation Book Code" := "Depreciation Book Code";
          InvPostingBuffer[1]."Depr. until FA Posting Date" := "Depr. until FA Posting Date";
          InvPostingBuffer[1]."Duplicate in Depreciation Book" := "Duplicate in Depreciation Book";
          InvPostingBuffer[1]."Use Duplication List" := "Use Duplication List";
        END;

        IF "VAT Calculation Type" = "VAT Calculation Type"::"Sales Tax" THEN BEGIN
          InvPostingBuffer[1]."Tax Area Code" := "Tax Area Code";
          InvPostingBuffer[1]."Tax Group Code" := "Tax Group Code";
          InvPostingBuffer[1]."Tax Liable" := "Tax Liable";
          InvPostingBuffer[1]."Use Tax" := FALSE;
          InvPostingBuffer[1].Quantity := "Qty. to Invoice (Base)";
        END;

        TempDocDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Sales Line");
        TempDocDim.SETRANGE("Line No.","Line No.");
        UpdInvPostingBuffer;

      END;
      // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE PostEcoTax@8053005(P_GLAccount@1000 : Code[20];P_EcoTaxAmount@1001 : Decimal;P_DimEntryNo@8053002 : Integer);
    VAR
      TempJnlLineDim@8053001 : TEMPORARY Record 356;
      TempDimBuf@8053000 : TEMPORARY Record 360;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
      WITH GenJnlLine DO BEGIN
        "Gen. Posting Type" := 0; // None
        "Gen. Bus. Posting Group" := '';
        "Gen. Prod. Posting Group" := '';
        "VAT Bus. Posting Group" := '';
        "VAT Prod. Posting Group" := '';
        "Depreciation Book Code" := '';
        "EU 3-Party Trade" := FALSE;
        "VAT Calculation Type" := "VAT Calculation Type"::"Normal VAT";
        "Bill-to/Pay-to No." := '';
        "VAT Amount" := 0;
        "Account No." := P_GLAccount;
        Amount := P_EcoTaxAmount;
        "Amount (LCY)" := 0;
        "Source Currency Code" := SalesHeader."Currency Code";
        "Source Curr. VAT Amount" := 0;
        "System-Created Entry" := TRUE;

        TempDimBuf.DELETEALL;
        TempJnlLineDim.DELETEALL;
        DimBufMgt.GetDimensions(P_DimEntryNo,TempDimBuf);
        DimMgt.CopyDimBufToJnlLineDim(
          TempDimBuf,TempJnlLineDim,GenJnlLine."Journal Template Name",
          GenJnlLine."Journal Batch Name",GenJnlLine."Line No.");
        GenJnlPostLine.RunWithCheck(GenJnlLine,TempJnlLineDim);
      END;
      // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetActivityCode@8053003(P_recSalesHeader@8053000 : Record 36) Result : Code[10];
    VAR
      L_recSalesLine@8053001 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
      Result := P_recSalesHeader."Activity Code";

      IF P_recSalesHeader."Activity Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
        L_recSalesLine.RESET;
        L_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type", P_recSalesHeader."Document Type");
        L_recSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.", P_recSalesHeader."No.");
        IF L_recSalesLine.FINDFIRST THEN
          Result := L_recSalesLine."Activity Code";
      END;

      EXIT(Result);
      // <-- 20110601-Wi00-CMC01.04
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE PostItemChargePerShptInv@8053004(SalesLine@1000000000 : Record 37);
    VAR
      SalesInvLine_L@1000000001 : Record 113;
      TempItemLedgEntry_L@1000000002 : TEMPORARY Record 32;
      ValueEntry_L@8053001 : Record 5802;
      SalesECDLedgerEntry_L@8053000 : Record 8053089;
      ItemTrackingMgt@1000000003 : Codeunit 6500;
      Factor@1000000004 : Decimal;
      NonDistrQuantity@1000000005 : Decimal;
      NonDistrQtyToAssign@1000000006 : Decimal;
      NonDistrAmountToAssign@1000000007 : Decimal;
      QtyToAssign@1000000008 : Decimal;
      AmountToAssign@1000000009 : Decimal;
      Sign@1000000010 : Decimal;
      DistributeCharge@1000000011 : Boolean;
      RecJob_L@8053002 : Record 167;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20111205-App05-CMC02.01.00-PRD01
      IF NOT SalesInvLine_L.GET(
        TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Applies-to Doc. No.",TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Applies-to Doc. Line No.")
      THEN
        ERROR(Text013);
      // --> 20140128-ANI01-CMC02.02.00-1350-BSA01
      IF SalesInvLine_L."Job No." <> '' THEN
        RecJob_L.GET(SalesInvLine_L."Job No.");
      IF NOT RecJob_L.Breeding THEN
      // <-- 20140128-ANI01-CMC02.02.00-1350-BSA01
        SalesInvLine_L.TESTFIELD("Job No.",'');
      IF SalesInvLine_L."Quantity (Base)" > 0 THEN
        Sign := 1
      ELSE
        Sign := -1;

      SalesECDLedgerEntry_L.GET(TempItemChargeAssgntSales."ECD Invoice Entry");
      ValueEntry_L.GET(SalesECDLedgerEntry_L."Value Entry No.");

      IF ValueEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry No." <> 0 THEN
        DistributeCharge :=
          CostCalcMgt.SplitItemLedgerEntriesExist(
            TempItemLedgEntry_L,-SalesInvLine_L."Quantity (Base)",ValueEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry No.");

      IF DistributeCharge THEN BEGIN
        TempItemLedgEntry_L.FINDSET;
        NonDistrQuantity := SalesInvLine_L."Quantity (Base)";
        NonDistrQtyToAssign := TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Qty. to Assign";
        NonDistrAmountToAssign := TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Amount to Assign";
        REPEAT
          Factor := ABS(TempItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity) / NonDistrQuantity;
          QtyToAssign := NonDistrQtyToAssign * Factor;
          AmountToAssign := ROUND(NonDistrAmountToAssign * Factor,GLSetup."Amount Rounding Precision");
          IF Factor < 1 THEN BEGIN
            PostItemCharge(SalesLine,
              TempItemLedgEntry_L."Entry No.",ABS(TempItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity),
              AmountToAssign,QtyToAssign);
            NonDistrQuantity := NonDistrQuantity - ABS(TempItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity);
            NonDistrQtyToAssign := NonDistrQtyToAssign - QtyToAssign;
            NonDistrAmountToAssign := NonDistrAmountToAssign - AmountToAssign;
          END ELSE // the last time
            PostItemCharge(SalesLine,
              TempItemLedgEntry_L."Entry No.",ABS(TempItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity),
              NonDistrAmountToAssign,NonDistrQtyToAssign);
        UNTIL TempItemLedgEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
      END ELSE
        PostItemCharge(SalesLine,
          ValueEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry No.",SalesInvLine_L."Quantity (Base)",
          TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Amount to Assign",
          TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Qty. to Assign");
      // <-- 20111205-App05-CMC02.01.00-PRD01
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE PostItemChargePerRetRcptCr@8053009(SalesLine@1000 : Record 37);
    VAR
      ReturnCrMemoLine_L@1000000000 : Record 115;
      TempItemLedgEntry_L@1000000001 : TEMPORARY Record 32;
      ValueEntry_L@8053001 : Record 5802;
      SalesECDLedgerEntry_L@8053000 : Record 8053089;
      ItemTrackingMgt@1000000002 : Codeunit 6500;
      Factor@1000000003 : Decimal;
      NonDistrQuantity@1000000004 : Decimal;
      NonDistrQtyToAssign@1000000005 : Decimal;
      NonDistrAmountToAssign@1000000006 : Decimal;
      QtyToAssign@1000000007 : Decimal;
      AmountToAssign@1000000008 : Decimal;
      Sign@8053002 : Decimal;
      DistributeCharge@1000000009 : Boolean;
      RecJob_L@8053003 : Record 167;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20111205-App05-CMC02.01.00-PRD01
      IF NOT ReturnCrMemoLine_L.GET(
        TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Applies-to Doc. No.",TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Applies-to Doc. Line No.")
      THEN
        ERROR(Text013);
      // --> 20140128-ANI01-CMC02.02.00-1350-BSA01
      IF ReturnCrMemoLine_L."Job No." <> '' THEN
        RecJob_L.GET(ReturnCrMemoLine_L."Job No.");
      IF NOT RecJob_L.Breeding THEN
      // <-- 20140128-ANI01-CMC02.02.00-1350-BSA01
        ReturnCrMemoLine_L.TESTFIELD("Job No.",'');
      IF ReturnCrMemoLine_L."Quantity (Base)" > 0 THEN
        Sign := 1
      ELSE
        Sign := -1;

      SalesECDLedgerEntry_L.GET(ItemChargeAssgntSales."ECD Invoice Entry");
      ValueEntry_L.GET(SalesECDLedgerEntry_L."Value Entry No.");

      IF ValueEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry No." <> 0 THEN
        DistributeCharge :=
          CostCalcMgt.SplitItemLedgerEntriesExist(
            TempItemLedgEntry_L,ReturnCrMemoLine_L."Quantity (Base)",ValueEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry No.");

      IF DistributeCharge THEN BEGIN
        TempItemLedgEntry_L.FINDSET;
        NonDistrQuantity := ReturnCrMemoLine_L."Quantity (Base)";
        NonDistrQtyToAssign := TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Qty. to Assign";
        NonDistrAmountToAssign := TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Amount to Assign";
        REPEAT
          Factor := ABS(TempItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity) / NonDistrQuantity;
          QtyToAssign := NonDistrQtyToAssign * Factor;
          AmountToAssign := ROUND(NonDistrAmountToAssign * Factor,GLSetup."Amount Rounding Precision");
          IF Factor < 1 THEN BEGIN
            PostItemCharge(SalesLine,
              TempItemLedgEntry_L."Entry No.",ABS(TempItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity),
              AmountToAssign,QtyToAssign);
            NonDistrQuantity := NonDistrQuantity - ABS(TempItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity);
            NonDistrQtyToAssign := NonDistrQtyToAssign - QtyToAssign;
            NonDistrAmountToAssign := NonDistrAmountToAssign - AmountToAssign;
          END ELSE // the last time
            PostItemCharge(SalesLine,
              TempItemLedgEntry_L."Entry No.",ABS(TempItemLedgEntry_L.Quantity),
              NonDistrAmountToAssign,NonDistrQtyToAssign);
        UNTIL TempItemLedgEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
      END ELSE
        PostItemCharge(SalesLine,
          ValueEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry No.",ReturnCrMemoLine_L."Quantity (Base)",
          TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Amount to Assign",
          TempItemChargeAssgntSales."Qty. to Assign")
      // <-- 20111205-App05-CMC02.01.00-PRD01
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSalesLine@8053015(VAR FromSalesLine_P@8053000 : TEMPORARY Record 37;VAR LinkedSalesLine_P@8053001 : Record 37);
    VAR
      TempLinkedSalesLine_L@8053004 : TEMPORARY Record 37;
      RecSalesHeader_L@8053002 : Record 36;
      QteToUpdate_L@8053013 : Decimal;
      QteMaxToDef_L@8053014 : Decimal;
      WhseSalesRelease_L@8053003 : Codeunit 5771;
      cduLogisticsTourMngt_L@8053005 : Codeunit 8052991;
      NegocprixToUpdate_L@8053006 : Boolean;
      recSalesLine_L@8053007 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20120624-Log03-CMC02.01.00-SD01
      QteMaxToDef_L := LinkedSalesLine_P.Quantity-LinkedSalesLine_P."Quantity Shipped";

      // MAJ de la ligne commande li'e

      LinkedSalesLine_P.FIND('=');

      TempLinkedSalesLine_L:=  LinkedSalesLine_P;
      LinkedSalesLine_P.SuspendStatusCheck(TRUE);
      FromSalesLine_P.SuspendStatusCheck(TRUE);
      IF FromSalesLine_P."Qty. to Ship" >= QteMaxToDef_L THEN
        QteToUpdate_L := QteMaxToDef_L
      ELSE
        QteToUpdate_L := FromSalesLine_P."Qty. to Ship";

      IF LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepayment %" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        FromSalesLine_P.VALIDATE("Prepmt. Line Amount",ROUND(LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Line Amount"/LinkedSalesLine_P.Quantity *
          QteToUpdate_L,0.01));
        LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Line Amount" := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Line Amount" - FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Line Amount";
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amt. Inv." := ROUND(LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amt. Inv." / LinkedSalesLine_P.Quantity *
          QteToUpdate_L,0.01);
        LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amt. Inv." := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amt. Inv." - FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amt. Inv.";
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amt. Incl. VAT" := ROUND(LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amt. Incl. VAT" / LinkedSalesLine_P.Quantity *
          QteToUpdate_L,0.01);
        LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amt. Incl. VAT" := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amt. Incl. VAT" -
          FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amt. Incl. VAT";
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepayment Amount" := ROUND(LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepayment Amount" / LinkedSalesLine_P.Quantity *
          QteToUpdate_L,0.01);
        LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepayment Amount" := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepayment Amount" - FromSalesLine_P."Prepayment Amount";
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. VAT Base Amt." := ROUND(LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. VAT Base Amt." / LinkedSalesLine_P.Quantity *
          QteToUpdate_L,0.01);
        LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. VAT Base Amt." := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. VAT Base Amt." - FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. VAT Base Amt."
      ;
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepayment VAT %" := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepayment VAT %" ;
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. VAT Calc. Type" := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. VAT Calc. Type";
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepayment VAT Identifier" := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepayment VAT Identifier";
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt Amt to Deduct" := FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Line Amount";
        LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt Amt to Deduct" := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Line Amount" - LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt Amt Deducted";
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt Amt Deducted" := 0;
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepayment Line" := FALSE;
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amount Inv. Incl. VAT" := ROUND(LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amount Inv. Incl. VAT" /
          LinkedSalesLine_P.Quantity* QteToUpdate_L,0.01);
        LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amount Inv. Incl. VAT" := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amount Inv. Incl. VAT" -
          FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amount Inv. Incl. VAT";
        FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amount Inv. (LCY)" := FromSalesLine_P."Prepayment Amount" - FromSalesLine_P."Prepmt Amt Deducted";
        LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Amount Inv. (LCY)" := LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepayment Amount" - LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt Amt Deducted"
      ;
        FromSalesLine_P.MODIFY;
      END;

      NegocprixToUpdate_L := FALSE;
      IF NOT LinkedSalesLine_P."Negotiated Price" THEN
        NegocprixToUpdate_L := TRUE;
      LinkedSalesLine_P."Negotiated Price" := TRUE;

      IF FromSalesLine_P."Qty. to Ship" < QteMaxToDef_L THEN
        LinkedSalesLine_P.VALIDATE(Quantity,LinkedSalesLine_P.Quantity - FromSalesLine_P."Qty. to Ship")
      ELSE
        LinkedSalesLine_P.VALIDATE(Quantity,LinkedSalesLine_P."Quantity Shipped");

      IF NegocprixToUpdate_L THEN
        LinkedSalesLine_P."Negotiated Price" := FALSE;
      LinkedSalesLine_P.VALIDATE("Unit Price",TempLinkedSalesLine_L."Unit Price");
      LinkedSalesLine_P.VALIDATE("Line Discount %",TempLinkedSalesLine_L."Line Discount %");

      LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt Amt to Deduct":=LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt. Line Amount"-LinkedSalesLine_P."Prepmt Amt Deducted";

      // --> 20130220-App12-CMC02.01.00-FDA
      // cas des gratuits
      // sur la ligne initiale
      IF LinkedSalesLine_P.Quantity= 0 THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.RESET;
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",LinkedSalesLine_P."Document Type");
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",LinkedSalesLine_P."Document No.");
        recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Free Item Root Line",LinkedSalesLine_P."Line No.");
        recSalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Outstanding Quantity",'<>0');
        IF recSalesLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN
           recSalesLine_L.DELETE(TRUE);
      END;
      // <-- 20130220-App12-CMC02.01.00-FDA

      LinkedSalesLine_P.MODIFY(TRUE);

      // --> 20130220-App12-CMC02.01.00-FDA
      // cas des gratuits
      // sur la ligne initiale
      recSalesLine_L.RESET;
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",LinkedSalesLine_P."Document Type");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",LinkedSalesLine_P."Document No.");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Free Item Root Line",LinkedSalesLine_P."Line No.");
      recSalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Outstanding Quantity",'<>0');
      IF recSalesLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity,ROUND(LinkedSalesLine_P.Quantity*recSalesLine_L."Free Item Qty Per",0.01));
        recSalesLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
      END;
      recSalesLine_L.RESET;
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",FromSalesLine_P."Document Type");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",FromSalesLine_P."Document No.");
      recSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Free Item Root Line",FromSalesLine_P."Line No.");
      recSalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Outstanding Quantity",'<>0');
      IF recSalesLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        recSalesLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity,ROUND(FromSalesLine_P.Quantity*recSalesLine_L."Free Item Qty Per",0.01));
        recSalesLine_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
      END;
      // <-- 20130220-App12-CMC02.01.00-FDA

      //Relancer la commande

      RecSalesHeader_L.GET(LinkedSalesLine_P."Document Type",LinkedSalesLine_P."Document No.");
      WhseSalesRelease_L.Release(RecSalesHeader_L);
      cduLogisticsTourMngt_L.Release(RecSalesHeader_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateJnlLineDiscount@8053012();
    VAR
      CMCSalesDocDiscount_L@8053000 : Record 8053177;
      SalesDiscount_L@8053001 : Record 8053174;
    BEGIN
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // The function fills a temporary table with journal lines discounts containing discounts for each line of the document.
      // Input:
      //   -
      // Output:
      //   -
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20130821-APP33-CMC02.02.00-BSI01
      CLEAR(CMCSalesDocDiscount_L);
      CMCSalesDocDiscount_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", SalesLine."Document Type");
      CMCSalesDocDiscount_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", SalesLine."Document No.");
      CMCSalesDocDiscount_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.", SalesLine."Line No.");
      IF CMCSalesDocDiscount_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          // --> 20150916-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2272-JCD01
          {BEFORE
          SalesDiscount_L.GET(CMCSalesDocDiscount_L."Discount Code");
          }//AFTER
          IF SalesDiscount_L.GET(CMCSalesDocDiscount_L."Discount Code") THEN
          // <-- 20150916-APP33-ADV01.01.00-2272-JCD01
          IF SalesDiscount_L.Type = SalesDiscount_L.Type::ECD THEN BEGIN
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Table ID"              := 37;
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Journal Template Name" := ItemJnlLine."Journal Template Name";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Journal Batch Name"    := ItemJnlLine."Journal Batch Name";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Journal Line No."      := ItemJnlLine."Line No.";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Discount Code"         := CMCSalesDocDiscount_L."Discount Code";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G.Rank                    := CMCSalesDocDiscount_L.Rank;
            // --> 20141020-APP33-TER01.01.00-FDA01
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Base Value %"            := CMCSalesDocDiscount_L."Base Value %";
            // <-- 20141020-APP33-TER01.01.00-FDA01
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Base Value"            := CMCSalesDocDiscount_L."Base Value";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Discount %"            := CMCSalesDocDiscount_L."Discount %";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Discount Amount"       := CMCSalesDocDiscount_L."Discount Amount";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Final Price"           := CMCSalesDocDiscount_L."Final Price";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G.INSERT(FALSE);
          END;
        UNTIL CMCSalesDocDiscount_L.NEXT=0;
      // <-- 20130821-APP33-CMC02.02.00-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE AutoSetOff@8053006(SalesHeader_P@8053000 : Record 36);
    VAR
      GenJnlLine_L@8053001 : Record 81;
      CMCSetup_L@8053002 : Record 8052982;
      TempJnlLineDim_L@8053003 : TEMPORARY Record 356;
      FarmerRec_L@8053004 : Record 5050;
      ContactBusRelation_L@8053005 : Record 5054;
      VendorNo_L@8053006 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      //-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Mouvement de compensation automatique des transactions si mode de rSglement compte d'posant
      // Entr'es
      //  - SalesHeaer_P : Record Sales Header
      //-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20130910-App29-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
      WITH SalesHeader_P DO BEGIN

        // ** Compte client --> Compte de contrepassation **//

        FindCustLedgEntry(CustLedgEntry);
        GenJnlLine.INIT;
        GenJnlLine."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
        GenJnlLine."Document Date" := "Document Date";
        GenJnlLine.Description := "Posting Description";
        GenJnlLine."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" := "Shortcut Dimension 1 Code";
        GenJnlLine."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" := "Shortcut Dimension 2 Code";
        GenJnlLine."Reason Code" := "Reason Code";
        GenJnlLine."Account Type" := GenJnlLine."Account Type"::Customer;
        GenJnlLine."Account No." := "Bill-to Customer No.";
        GenJnlLine."Document Type" := GenJnlLine."Document Type"::Payment;
        GenJnlLine."Document No." := GenJnlLineDocNo;
        GenJnlLine."External Document No." := GenJnlLineExtDocNo;
        // --> 20160330-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2882-JCD01
        GenJnlLine."Due Date" := DueDate_G;
        // <-- 20160330-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2882-JCD01
        CMCSetup_L.GET;
        CMCSetup_L.TESTFIELD("SettOff Bal Acount");
        GenJnlLine."Bal. Account No." := CMCSetup_L."SettOff Bal Acount";
        GenJnlLine."Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
        GenJnlLine.VALIDATE("Debit Amount",TotalSalesLine."Amount Including VAT");
        GenJnlLine."Source Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
        GenJnlLine."Source Currency Amount" := GenJnlLine.Amount;
        GenJnlLine.Correction := Correction;
        IF "Currency Code" = '' THEN
          GenJnlLine."Currency Factor" := 1
        ELSE
          GenJnlLine."Currency Factor" := "Currency Factor";
        GenJnlLine."Applies-to Doc. Type" := GenJnlLineDocType;
        GenJnlLine."Applies-to Doc. No." := GenJnlLineDocNo;
        GenJnlLine."Source Type" := GenJnlLine."Source Type"::Customer;
        GenJnlLine."Source No." := "Bill-to Customer No.";
        GenJnlLine."Source Code" := SrcCode;
        GenJnlLine."Posting No. Series" := "Posting No. Series";
        GenJnlLine."IC Partner Code" := "Sell-to IC Partner Code";
        GenJnlLine."Allow Zero-Amount Posting" := TRUE;
        NewFieldsManagement_G.GenJournalLine_SalesHeader(GenJnlLine,SalesHeader);
        GenJnlPostLine.RunWithCheck(GenJnlLine,TempJnlLineDim_L);

        // ** Compte de contrepassation --> fournisseur **//

        FarmerRec_L.RESET;
        FarmerRec_L.SETRANGE(Type,FarmerRec_L.Type::Company);
        FarmerRec_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.","Bill-to Customer No.");
        IF FarmerRec_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          ContactBusRelation_L.RESET;
          ContactBusRelation_L.SETRANGE(ContactBusRelation_L."Contact No.",FarmerRec_L."No.");
          ContactBusRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table",ContactBusRelation_L."Link to Table"::Vendor);
          IF ContactBusRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN
            VendorNo_L := ContactBusRelation_L."No.";
        END;
        GenJnlLine.INIT;
        GenJnlLine."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
        GenJnlLine."Document Date" := "Document Date";
        GenJnlLine.Description := "Posting Description";
        GenJnlLine."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" := "Shortcut Dimension 1 Code";
        GenJnlLine."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" := "Shortcut Dimension 2 Code";
        GenJnlLine."Reason Code" := "Reason Code";
        GenJnlLine."Account No." := CMCSetup_L."SettOff Bal Acount";
        GenJnlLine."Document Type" := GenJnlLine."Document Type"::Payment;
        GenJnlLine."Document No." := GenJnlLineDocNo;
        GenJnlLine."External Document No." := GenJnlLineExtDocNo;
        // --> 20160330-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2882-JCD01
        GenJnlLine."Due Date" := DueDate_G;
        // <-- 20160330-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2882-JCD01
        GenJnlLine."Bal. Account Type" := GenJnlLine."Bal. Account Type"::Vendor;
        GenJnlLine."Bal. Account No." := VendorNo_L;
        GenJnlLine."Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
        GenJnlLine.VALIDATE("Debit Amount",TotalSalesLine."Amount Including VAT");
        GenJnlLine."Source Currency Code" := "Currency Code";
        GenJnlLine."Source Currency Amount" := GenJnlLine.Amount;
        GenJnlLine."Source Code" := SrcCode;
        GenJnlLine.Correction := Correction;
        IF "Currency Code" = '' THEN
          GenJnlLine."Currency Factor" := 1
        ELSE
          GenJnlLine."Currency Factor" := "Currency Factor";
        GenJnlLine."Posting No. Series" := "Posting No. Series";
        GenJnlLine."IC Partner Code" := "Sell-to IC Partner Code";
        GenJnlLine."Allow Zero-Amount Posting" := TRUE;
        NewFieldsManagement_G.GenJournalLine_SalesHeader(GenJnlLine,SalesHeader);
        GenJnlPostLine.RunWithCheck(GenJnlLine,TempJnlLineDim_L);
      END;
      // <-- 20130910-App29-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetPrepmtNo@8053021(ShipmtNo_P@8053000 : Code[20];ShipmtLineNo_p@8053001 : Integer) Res_PrepmtNo : Code[20];
    VAR
      SalesShipmentLine_L@8053002 : Record 111;
      SalesLine_L@8053003 : Record 37;
      SalesInvoiceHeader_L@8053004 : Record 112;
    BEGIN
      //-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //
      // Recherche du N› de facture d'acompte en tenant compte des enlSvements
      //
      // Entr'es
      //    - ShipmtNo_P : N› exp'dition
      //    - ShipmtLineNo_p : N› ligne exp'dition
      // Sortie
      //    - Res_PrepmtNo : le N› acompte sinon ''
      //-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20131009-Log03-CMC02.01.00-0922-DAD
      WITH SalesShipmentLine_L DO BEGIN
        IF GET(ShipmtNo_P,ShipmtLineNo_p) THEN BEGIN
          IF SalesLine_L.GET(SalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order,"Order No.","Order Line No.") THEN BEGIN
            IF SalesLine_L."Linked Order No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
              SalesInvoiceHeader_L.SETRANGE("Prepayment Order No.",SalesLine_L."Linked Order No.");
              IF SalesInvoiceHeader_L.FIND('+') THEN BEGIN
                Res_PrepmtNo := SalesInvoiceHeader_L."No.";
                EXIT;
              END;
            END;
            SalesInvoiceHeader_L.SETRANGE("Prepayment Order No.","Order No.");
            IF SalesInvoiceHeader_L.FINDLAST THEN
              Res_PrepmtNo := SalesInvoiceHeader_L."No." ;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 20131009-Log03-CMC02.01.00-0922-DAD
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckCellMandatory@8053007(VAR SalesLine_P@1000 : Record 37;TypeMvt_P@8053002 : 'Receive,Ship');
    VAR
      SalesLine2_L@1001 : Record 37;
      CduItemJnlPostLine_L@8053000 : Codeunit 22;
      recItem_L@8053001 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CheckCellMandatory
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Input:
      //   - SalesLine_P
      //   - TypeMvt_P
      // Output:
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20140105-WI00-CMC02.02.00-1386-SD01
      SalesLine2_L.COPY(SalesLine_P);
      SalesLine2_L.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLine2_L.Type::Item);
      SalesLine2_L.SETRANGE("Drop Shipment",FALSE);
      CASE TypeMvt_P OF
        TypeMvt_P::Ship :SalesLine2_L.SETFILTER("Qty. to Ship",'<>0');
        TypeMvt_P::Receive :SalesLine2_L.SETFILTER("Return Qty. to Receive",'<>0');
      END;
      IF SalesLine2_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          GetLocation(SalesLine2_L."Location Code");
          recItem_L.GET(SalesLine2_L."No.");
          IF CduItemJnlPostLine_L.ItemToReverse(recItem_L,Location."Cell Item Filter View") THEN BEGIN
            SalesLine2_L.TESTFIELD("Cell Code")
          END ELSE
            SalesLine2_L.TESTFIELD("Cell Code",'');
        UNTIL SalesLine2_L.NEXT = 0;
      // <-- 20140105-WI00-CMC02.02.00-1386-SD01
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSourceOrder@1000000000(SalesLine_P@1000000000 : Record 37);
    VAR
      SalesOrderLine_L@1000000001 : Record 37;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140513-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2107-FEC01
      IF (SalesLine_P."Source Order No." <> '') AND
         (SalesOrderLine_L.GET(SalesOrderLine_L."Document Type"::Order,
         SalesLine_P."Source Order No.",SalesLine_P."Source Order Line No."))
      THEN BEGIN
        SalesOrderLine_L.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);
        SalesOrderLine_L.SuspendStatusCheck(TRUE);
        SalesOrderLine_L.VALIDATE(Quantity,SalesOrderLine_L.Quantity + SalesLine_P."Return Qty. to Receive");
        SalesOrderLine_L.MODIFY;
      END;
      // <-- 20140513-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-2107-FEC01
    END;

    PROCEDURE PostIncropoMvt@1000000001(SalesLine_P@1000000000 : Record 37);
    VAR
      RecItem_L@1000000001 : Record 27;
      RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L@1000000002 : Record 8053231;
      RecItemJnlLine_L@1000000003 : Record 83;
      RecSalesHeader_L@1000000004 : Record 36;
      RecItemCereal_L@1000000005 : Record 27;
      L_PostItemJnlLine@1000000006 : Codeunit 22;
      L_TempJnlLineDim@1000000007 : TEMPORARY Record 356;
      CduCMDimensions_L@8053000 : Codeunit 8053004;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140528-APP28-CMC02.02.00-2108-BSI01
      RecSalesHeader_L.GET(SalesLine_P."Document Type",SalesLine_P."Document No.");
      RecItem_L.GET(SalesLine_P."No.");
      IF RecItem_L.TAF THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L);
        RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.",SalesLine_P."CMC Campaign No.");
        RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L.SETRANGE("Item No.",SalesLine_P."No.");
        RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date",'<=%1',SalesLine_P."Shipment Date");
        RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L.SETFILTER("Ending Date",'>=%1',SalesLine_P."Shipment Date");
        IF RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            RecItemCereal_L.GET(RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L."Cereal Item No.");
            RecItemJnlLine_L.INIT;
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Posting Date" := RecSalesHeader_L."Posting Date";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Document Date" := RecSalesHeader_L."Document Date";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Document No." := SalesLine_P."Document No.";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Item No." := RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L."Cereal Item No.";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Variant Code" := RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L."Variant Code";

            RecItemJnlLine_L."Collection Type" := RecItemJnlLine_L."Collection Type"::Purchase;
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Movement Type" := RecItemJnlLine_L."Movement Type"::E05;
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Stock Type" := RecItemJnlLine_L."Stock Type"::Company;
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Location Code" := SalesLine_P."Location Code";
            IF  ABS(SalesLine_P."Qty. to Ship")>0 THEN BEGIN
              RecItemJnlLine_L."Entry Type" := RecItemJnlLine_L."Entry Type"::"Negative Adjmt.";
            RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity := ABS((SalesLine_P."Qty. to Ship" * RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L."Incorp. Rate")/100);
            END;
            IF ABS(SalesLine_P."Return Qty. to Receive")>0 THEN BEGIN
              RecItemJnlLine_L."Entry Type" := RecItemJnlLine_L."Entry Type"::"Positive Adjmt.";
              RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity := ABS((SalesLine_P."Return Qty. to Receive" * RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L."Incorp. Rate")/100);
            END;

            RecItemJnlLine_L."Invoiced Quantity" := RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity;
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Quantity (Base)" := RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity;
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Invoiced Qty. (Base)" := RecItemJnlLine_L.Quantity;
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" := SalesLine_P."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."New Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" := SalesLine_P."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" := SalesLine_P."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."New Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" := SalesLine_P."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Gen. Prod. Posting Group" := RecItemCereal_L."Gen. Prod. Posting Group";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Inventory Posting Group" := RecItemCereal_L."Inventory Posting Group";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Unit of Measure Code" := RecItemCereal_L."Base Unit of Measure";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Qty. per Unit of Measure" := 1;
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Product Group Code" := RecItemCereal_L."Product Group Code";
            RecItemJnlLine_L."Item Category Code" := RecItemCereal_L."Item Category Code";
            RecItemJnlLine_L.VALIDATE("Campaign No.",SalesLine_P."CMC Campaign No.");
            // --> 20150520-APP28-CMC02.02.00-3004-FEC01
            {BEFORE
            CLEAR(L_PostItemJnlLine);
            L_PostItemJnlLine.SetBonisAdjustment(TRUE);
            L_PostItemJnlLine.SetCheckCell(TRUE);
            L_PostItemJnlLine.SetCellMandatory(TRUE);
            CduCMDimensions_L.DimFromItemJnl(L_TempJnlLineDim,RecItemJnlLine_L,TRUE);
            L_PostItemJnlLine.RunWithCheck(RecItemJnlLine_L,L_TempJnlLineDim);
            AFTER}
            ItemJnlPostLine.SetBonisAdjustment(TRUE);
            ItemJnlPostLine.SetCheckCell(TRUE);
            ItemJnlPostLine.SetCellMandatory(TRUE);
            CduCMDimensions_L.DimFromItemJnl(L_TempJnlLineDim,RecItemJnlLine_L,TRUE);
            ItemJnlPostLine.RunWithCheck(RecItemJnlLine_L,L_TempJnlLineDim);
            // <-- 20150520-APP28-CMC02.02.00-3004-FEC01
          UNTIL RecItemCerealIncorpRate_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      // <-- 20140528-APP28-CMC02.02.00-2108-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateGLDescription@8053008(Rec_P@8053000 : Record 36;VAR Description_P@8053001 : Text[50]);
    VAR
      CMCActivity_L@8053003 : Record 8052985;
      CMCFamily_L@8053004 : Record 8052986;
      CMCSubFamily_L@8053005 : Record 8052987;
      CMCSpecies_L@8053006 : Record 8052988;
      SalesLine_L@8053007 : Record 37;
      Item_L@8053009 : Record 27;
      PaymentMethod_L@8053002 : Record 289;
      ActivityTXT_L@8053008 : Text[50];
      ApportTxt_L@8053011 : TextConst 'ENU=Apport;FRA=Apport';
      LibDocTXT_L@1000000000 : Text[200];
      TypeContratCadre_L@1000000002 : Record 8053198;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140609-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-AKH01
      WITH Rec_P DO BEGIN
        // --> 20150227-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-2793-BRA01
        //BEFORE
        {
        IF NOT ("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::Invoice,"Document Type"::"Credit Memo"]) THEN
          EXIT;
        }
        // <-- 20150227-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-2793-BRA01

        SalesSetup.GET;
        IF SalesSetup."G/L Label"='' THEN
          EXIT;
        SalesLine_L.RESET;
        SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
        SalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
        SalesLine_L.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLine_L.Type::Item);
        IF SalesLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          Item_L.GET(SalesLine_L."No.");
          CASE  TRUE OF
            (CMCSpecies_L.GET(SalesLine_L."Species Code") AND (CMCSpecies_L."Sales G/L Label" <> '')) :
              ActivityTXT_L := CMCSpecies_L."Sales G/L Label";
            (CMCSubFamily_L.GET(SalesLine_L."Sub-Family Code") AND (CMCSubFamily_L."Sales G/L Label" <> '')):
              ActivityTXT_L := CMCSubFamily_L."Sales G/L Label";
            (CMCFamily_L.GET(SalesLine_L."Family Code") AND (CMCFamily_L."Sales G/L Label" <> '')):
              ActivityTXT_L := CMCFamily_L."Sales G/L Label";
            (CMCActivity_L.GET(SalesLine_L."Activity Code") AND (CMCActivity_L."Sales G/L Label" <> '')):
              ActivityTXT_L := CMCActivity_L."Sales G/L Label";
          END;
        END ELSE
          ActivityTXT_L := '';

        IF NOT PaymentMethod_L.GET("Payment Method Code") THEN
          CLEAR(PaymentMethod_L);
        // --> 20150227-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-2793-BRA01
        //BEFORE
        //Description_P := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(SalesSetup."G/L Label",FORMAT("Document Type"),GenJnlLineDocNo,ActivityTXT_L,
        //AFTER
        // --> 20151216-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2553-JCD01
        {BEFORE
        Description_P := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(SalesSetup."G/L Label",FORMAT(GenJnlLine."Document Type"),GenJnlLineDocNo,ActivityTXT_L,
        }//AFTER
        IF Rec_P."CMC Prepayment" THEN BEGIN
          LibDocTXT_L := Text8053007;
          // --> 20260921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-48032-SAN01
          CASE GenJnlLine."Document Type" OF
              GenJnlLine."Document Type"::Invoice :  IF SalesSetup."Substitution PrePay. Invoice" <> '' THEN
                  LibDocTXT_L := SalesSetup."Substitution PrePay. Invoice";

              GenJnlLine."Document Type"::"Credit Memo" :  IF SalesSetup."Substitution PrePay. CrMemo" <> '' THEN
                  LibDocTXT_L := SalesSetup."Substitution PrePay. CrMemo";
          END;
          // <-- 20260921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-48032-SAN01
        END ELSE
          LibDocTXT_L := FORMAT(GenJnlLine."Document Type");

        // --> 20160531-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2976-CJY01
        IF (("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Credit Memo") AND
          ("Doc. Category Code" <> '')) THEN BEGIN
              TypeContratCadre_L.RESET;
              TypeContratCadre_L.SETFILTER(Type,'%1|%2',TypeContratCadre_L.Type::CCA,TypeContratCadre_L.Type::JA);
              TypeContratCadre_L.SETRANGE("Doc. Category Code","Doc. Category Code");
              IF TypeContratCadre_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                IF TypeContratCadre_L."Designation comptable vente" <> '' THEN
                  ActivityTXT_L := TypeContratCadre_L."Designation comptable vente";
              END;

        END;
        // <-- 20160531-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2976-CJY01

        Description_P := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(SalesSetup."G/L Label",LibDocTXT_L,GenJnlLineDocNo,ActivityTXT_L,
        // <-- 20151216-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2553-JCD01
        // <-- 20150227-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-2793-BRA01
          PaymentMethod_L.Description),1,50);
      END;
      // <-- 20140609-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-AKH01
    END;

      ItemJnlLine."Drop Shipment" := PurchOrderLine."Drop Shipment";

      ItemJnlLine."Document Date" := SalesHeader."Document Date";

              ItemEntryRelation.INSERT;
            UNTIL TempHandlingSpecification2.NEXT = 0;
          EXIT(0);
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(ItemJnlLine."Item Shpt. Entry No.");
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateReferencePrice@60000(SalesContractNo_P@60000 : Code[20];DocumentNo_P@60002 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      CMCSalesContract_L@60001 : Record 8053012;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20141218-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
      // This function will force the update of the reference price calculated
      // Input
      //    SalesContractNo_P
      // Output
      IF SalesContractNo_P = '' THEN
        EXIT;

      CLEAR(CMCSalesContract_L);
      CMCSalesContract_L.GET(SalesContractNo_P);
      IF CMCSalesContract_L.Status <> CMCSalesContract_L.Status::Closed THEN
        EXIT;

      // --> 20150115-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
      SalesHeader.CALCFIELDS(Amount);
      CMCSalesContract_L.SetInvoiceNo(DocumentNo_P,SalesHeader.Amount);
      // <-- 20150115-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
      CMCSalesContract_L.FillReferencePriceDetTable;
    END;

    PROCEDURE PostSalesVariatUnitPrice@1000000003(SalesLine_P@1000000001 : Record 37;PostDocNo_P@1000000000 : Code[20];PostDocLineNo_P@1000000005 : Integer);
    VAR
      "<<-- 3LI LOCALS -->>"@1000000004 : Integer;
      CMCSalesVariatPrice_L@1000000003 : Record 60053;
      CMCSalesVariatPrice2_L@1000000002 : Record 60053;
      LineNo_L@1000000006 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20150409-CER98-TER01.01.00-058-RMZ01
      // This function will create the lines for the posted document that as sales variat unit price
      // Input
      //    - SalesLine_P
      //    - PostDocNo_P
      //    - PostDocLineNo_P
      IF NOT (SalesLine_P."Document Type" IN [SalesLine_P."Document Type"::Invoice,SalesLine_P."Document Type"::"Credit Memo"]) THEN
        EXIT;

      LineNo_L := 10000;
      CLEAR(CMCSalesVariatPrice_L);
      IF SalesLine_P."Document Type" = SalesLine_P."Document Type"::Invoice THEN
        CMCSalesVariatPrice_L."Document Type" := CMCSalesVariatPrice_L."Document Type"::Invoice
      ELSE
        CMCSalesVariatPrice_L."Document Type" := CMCSalesVariatPrice_L."Document Type"::"Credit Memo";
      CMCSalesVariatPrice_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesLine_P."Document No.");
      CMCSalesVariatPrice_L.SETRANGE("Sales Line No.",SalesLine_P."Line No.");
      IF CMCSalesVariatPrice_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        REPEAT
          CLEAR(CMCSalesVariatPrice2_L);
          CMCSalesVariatPrice2_L := CMCSalesVariatPrice_L;
          IF SalesLine_P."Document Type" = SalesLine_P."Document Type"::Invoice THEN
            CMCSalesVariatPrice2_L."Document Type" := CMCSalesVariatPrice_L."Document Type"::"Posted invoice"
          ELSE
            CMCSalesVariatPrice2_L."Document Type" := CMCSalesVariatPrice_L."Document Type"::"Posted Credit Memo";

          CMCSalesVariatPrice2_L."Document No." := PostDocNo_P;
          CMCSalesVariatPrice2_L."Sales Line No." := PostDocLineNo_P;
          CMCSalesVariatPrice2_L."Line No." := LineNo_L;
          CMCSalesVariatPrice2_L.INSERT;
          LineNo_L := LineNo_L + 10000;
        UNTIL CMCSalesVariatPrice_L.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateMatifInvoicing@1000000004(SalesLine_P@1000000000 : Record 37);
    VAR
      "<<---- 3LI LOCALS ---->>"@1000000001 : Integer;
      CMCSetup_L@1000000004 : Record 8052982;
      MatifInvLink_L@1000000002 : Record 60064;
      MatifInvLink2_L@1000000003 : Record 60064;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20151012-CER126-TER01.01.00-2365-RMZ01
      // This function will update the table Matif Invoicing Link
      // Input
      //  - SalesLine_P - the ligne of the sales document
      // Output
      IF (SalesLine_P."No." = '') OR NOT (SalesLine_P."Document Type" IN [SalesLine_P."Document Type"::Invoice,
              SalesLine_P."Document Type"::"Credit Memo"]) THEN
        EXIT;

      CMCSetup_L.GET;
      CLEAR(MatifInvLink_L);
      MatifInvLink_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type","Document No.","Document Line No.");
      IF SalesLine_P."Document Type" = SalesLine_P."Document Type"::Invoice THEN
        MatifInvLink_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",MatifInvLink_L."Document Type"::Invoice)
      ELSE
        MatifInvLink_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",MatifInvLink_L."Document Type"::"Credit Memo");
      MatifInvLink_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesLine_P."Document No.");
      MatifInvLink_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",SalesLine_P."Line No.");
      IF MatifInvLink_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          MatifInvLink2_L.GET(MatifInvLink_L.Way,MatifInvLink_L."Contract No.",MatifInvLink_L."Line No.");
          IF SalesLine_P."Document Type" = SalesLine_P."Document Type"::Invoice THEN BEGIN
            MatifInvLink2_L."Document No." := SalesInvHeader."No.";
            MatifInvLink2_L."Document Type" := MatifInvLink2_L."Document Type"::"Posted Invoice";
            MatifInvLink2_L.Quantity := SalesLine_P.Quantity / CMCSetup_L."MATIF Weight per Lot";
          END
          ELSE BEGIN
            // --> 20151020-CER126-TER01.01.00-2365-FBE01
            //MatifInvLink2_L."Document No." := SalesCrMemoLine."No.";
            MatifInvLink2_L."Document No." := SalesCrMemoHeader."No.";
            // <-- 20151020-CER126-TER01.01.00-2365-FBE01
            MatifInvLink2_L."Document Type" := MatifInvLink2_L."Document Type"::"Posted Cr. Memo";
            // --> 20151020-CER126-TER01.01.00-2365-FBE01
            //MatifInvLink2_L.Quantity := -SalesLine_P.Quantity / CMCSetup_L."MATIF Weight per Lot";
            MatifInvLink2_L.Quantity := SalesLine_P.Quantity / CMCSetup_L."MATIF Weight per Lot";
            // <-- 20151020-CER126-TER01.01.00-2365-FBE01
          END;
          MatifInvLink2_L.MODIFY;
        UNTIL MatifInvLink_L.NEXT = 0;
      // <-- 20151012-CER126-TER01.01.00-2365-RMZ01
    END;

    PROCEDURE PostBOMJnlLineDesassembly@60002(VAR ItemJnlLine@1462000 : Record 83;SalesLine@1462001 : Record 37);
    VAR
      BOMJnlLine@1462002 : Record 89;
      TempKitSalesLine@1462003 : TEMPORARY Record 25000;
      TempKitBOMJnlLine@1462004 : TEMPORARY Record 25001;
      TempJnlLineDim@1462005 : TEMPORARY Record 356;
      ItemJnlLine2@1462006 : Record 83;
      TempHandlingSpecification@1462007 : TEMPORARY Record 336;
      KitManagement@1462008 : Codeunit 25000;
      BOMJnlPostLine@1462009 : Codeunit 32;
      Item_L@60000 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20171207-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
      //Bas'e sur la cope de la fonction standard PostBOMJnlLine mais pour g'rer les retours avec kit constitu' cette fois ci

      //On ne gSre que si l'article en quesion est param'tr' pour :
      Item_L.GET(ItemJnlLine."Item No.");
      IF Item_L."No return for kit component" THEN
        EXIT;

      BOMJnlLine.INIT;
      WITH ItemJnlLine DO BEGIN
        BOMJnlLine."Item No." := "Item No.";
        BOMJnlLine."Posting Date" := "Posting Date";
        BOMJnlLine."Document No." := "Document No.";
        BOMJnlLine.Description := Description;
        BOMJnlLine."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" := SalesLine."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code";
        BOMJnlLine."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" := SalesLine."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code";
        BOMJnlLine."Location Code" := "Location Code";
        BOMJnlLine.Quantity := Quantity;
        BOMJnlLine."Unit Cost" := "Unit Cost";
        BOMJnlLine."Total Cost" := Amount;
        BOMJnlLine."Inventory Posting Group" := "Inventory Posting Group";
        BOMJnlLine."Source Code" := "Source Code";
        BOMJnlLine."Reason Code" := "Reason Code";
        BOMJnlLine."Document Date" := "Document Date";
        // --> 20180115-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
        {BEFORE
        BOMJnlLine."External Document No." := STRSUBSTNO('%1',ItemJnlLine."Item Shpt. Entry No.");
        AFTER}

        BOMJnlLine."item ship entry no." := ItemJnlLine."Item Shpt. Entry No.";

        // <-- 20180115-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
        BOMJnlLine."Posting No. Series" := "Posting No. Series";
        BOMJnlLine."Variant Code" := "Variant Code";
        BOMJnlLine."Bin Code" := "Bin Code";
        BOMJnlLine."Qty. per Unit of Measure" := "Qty. per Unit of Measure";
        BOMJnlLine."Unit of Measure Code" := "Unit of Measure Code";
        BOMJnlLine."Quantity (Base)" := "Quantity (Base)";
        //BOMJnlLine."Entry Type" := BOMJnlLine."Entry Type"::Assembly;
        IF SalesLine."Document Type" =SalesLine."Document Type"::"Return Order"  THEN BEGIN
          BOMJnlLine."Entry Type" := BOMJnlLine."Entry Type"::Disassembly ;
          BOMJnlLine.Quantity := -1 * BOMJnlLine.Quantity;
          BOMJnlLine."Quantity (Base)" := -1 * BOMJnlLine."Quantity (Base)";
        END;
        // --> 20230523-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3623-CJY01
        BOMJnlLine."Cell Code" :=  SalesLine."Cell Code";
        // <-- 20230523-WI00-ADV01.01.00-SP3623-CJY01

        BOMJnlLine."Gen. Bus. Posting Group" := SalesHeader ."Gen. Bus. Posting Group";

        TempKitSalesLine.DELETEALL;
        KitManagement.GetKitSales(SalesLine,TempKitSalesLine);
        SalesLine."Quantity (Base)" := SalesLine."Return Qty. to Receive (Base)";
         // SalesLine."Quantity (Base)" := -SalesLine."Qty. to Ship (Base)";
        KitManagement.UpdateKitSales(SalesLine,TempKitSalesLine);
        TempKitBOMJnlLine.DELETEALL;
        // --> 20180115-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY02
        KitManagement.TakeUnitCost(TRUE);
        // <-- 20180115-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY02
        KitManagement.CopyKitSalesToKitBOM(TempKitSalesLine,TempKitBOMJnlLine);

        TempJnlLineDim.DELETEALL;
        TempDocDim.RESET;
        TempDocDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Sales Line");
        TempDocDim.SETRANGE("Line No.",SalesLine."Line No.");
        DimMgt.CopyDocDimToJnlLineDim(TempDocDim,TempJnlLineDim);

        ItemJnlLine2 := ItemJnlLine;
        ItemJnlLine2."Entry Type" := ItemJnlLine2."Entry Type"::"Negative Adjmt.";
        KitManagement.CopyKitResv(ItemJnlLine);

        KitManagement.CopyCompResv(TempKitSalesLine,TempKitBOMJnlLine);
        // --> 20171226-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
        BOMJnlPostLine.SetReturnID(ReturnRcptHeader."No.",ItemJnlLine2."Document Line No.");
        // <-- 20171226-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
        BOMJnlPostLine.RunWithCheck(BOMJnlLine,TempJnlLineDim,TempKitBOMJnlLine);
        BOMJnlPostLine.GetItemTracking(TempHandlingSpecification);
        KitManagement.UpdateKitResv(ItemJnlLine,TempHandlingSpecification);
        //IF BOMJnlLine."External Document No." <> '' THEN
          //EVALUATE("Applies-to Entry",BOMJnlLine."External Document No.");
        CLEAR(ItemJnlPostLine);
        IF WhseShip THEN
          WhsePostShpt.ClearWhseJnl;
      END;
      // <-- 20171207-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3286-CJY01
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateJnlLineDiscountPurchLd@60006();
    VAR
      CMCPurchDocDiscount_L@8053000 : Record 8053249;
      PurchDiscount_L@8053001 : Record 8053247;
    BEGIN
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cette fonction est reprise du Cu90 pour g'rer la partie achat en LD dans ce cu80
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      // --> 20190312-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33271-CJY01
      CLEAR(CMCPurchDocDiscount_L);
      CMCPurchDocDiscount_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", PurchOrderLine."Document Type");
      CMCPurchDocDiscount_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", PurchOrderLine."Document No.");
      CMCPurchDocDiscount_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.", PurchOrderLine."Line No.");

      IF CMCPurchDocDiscount_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        REPEAT
          PurchDiscount_L.GET(CMCPurchDocDiscount_L."Discount Code");
          IF PurchDiscount_L.Type = PurchDiscount_L.Type::ECD THEN BEGIN
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Table ID" := 39;
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Journal Template Name" := ItemJnlLine."Journal Template Name";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Journal Batch Name" := ItemJnlLine."Journal Batch Name";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Journal Line No." := ItemJnlLine."Line No.";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Discount Code" := CMCPurchDocDiscount_L."Discount Code";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G.Rank := CMCPurchDocDiscount_L.Rank;
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Base Value" := CMCPurchDocDiscount_L."Base Value";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Discount %" := CMCPurchDocDiscount_L."Discount %";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Discount Amount" := CMCPurchDocDiscount_L."Discount Amount";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."Final Price" := CMCPurchDocDiscount_L."Final Price";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G."ECD Vendor No." := CMCPurchDocDiscount_L."ECD Vendor No.";
            TempCMCJnlLineDisc_G.INSERT(FALSE);
          END;
        UNTIL CMCPurchDocDiscount_L.NEXT=0;
      // <-- 20190312-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33271-CJY01
    END;

    PROCEDURE GeneratePurchOrderCVOLine@60005(PurchRcptLine_P@60000 : Record 121;PurchOrderLine_P@60002 : Record 39);
    VAR
      PurchOrderLine_L@60001 : Record 39;
      PurchRcptLine_L@60003 : Record 121;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20190520-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3375-MRU01
      //V'rification s'il existe une ligne de CVO associ'e
      PurchOrderLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", PurchOrderLine_P."Document Type");
      PurchOrderLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", PurchOrderLine_P."Document No.");
      PurchOrderLine_L.SETRANGE("CVO Item Attached Line", PurchOrderLine_P."Line No.");
      IF PurchOrderLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        PurchRcptLine_L.INIT;
        PurchRcptLine_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(PurchOrderLine_L);
        PurchRcptLine_L."Posting Date" := PurchRcptLine_P."Posting Date";
        PurchRcptLine_L."Document No." := PurchRcptLine_P."Document No.";
        PurchRcptLine_L.Quantity := PurchRcptLine_P.Quantity;
        PurchRcptLine_L."Quantity (Base)" := PurchRcptLine_P."Quantity (Base)";
        PurchRcptLine_L."Quantity Invoiced" := 0;
        PurchRcptLine_L."Qty. Invoiced (Base)" := 0;
        PurchRcptLine_L."Order No." := PurchOrderLine_L."Document No.";
        PurchRcptLine_L."Order Line No." := PurchOrderLine_L."Line No.";
        PurchRcptLine_L."Qty. Rcd. Not Invoiced" :=
          PurchRcptLine_L.Quantity - PurchRcptLine_L."Quantity Invoiced";

        IF PurchRcptLine_L.Quantity <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          PurchRcptLine_L."Item Rcpt. Entry No." := PurchRcptLine_P."Item Rcpt. Entry No.";
          PurchRcptLine_L."Item Charge Base Amount" := PurchOrderLine_L."Line Amount"
        END;
        PurchRcptLine_L."DS Sell-to Customer No." := PurchRcptLine_P."DS Sell-to Customer No.";
        PurchRcptLine_L."DS Sell-to Customer Name" := PurchRcptLine_P."DS Sell-to Customer Name";
        PurchRcptLine_L."Vendor Shipment No." := PurchRcptLine_P."Vendor Shipment No.";
        PurchRcptLine_L."Vendor Order No." := PurchRcptLine_P."Vendor Order No.";
        PurchRcptLine_L.INSERT;
        PurchOrderLine_L."Qty. to Receive" := PurchRcptLine_P.Quantity;
        PurchOrderLine_L."Qty. to Receive (Base)" := PurchRcptLine_P."Quantity (Base)";
        PurchPost.UpdateBlanketOrderLine(PurchOrderLine_L,TRUE,FALSE,FALSE);
      END;
      // <-- 20190520-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3375-MRU01
    END;

    PROCEDURE PostResAssgnementAcp@1000000005(SalesInvLine_P@1000000000 : Record 113;SalesCrMemoLine_P@1000000011 : Record 115;TempSalesLine_P@1000000001 : TEMPORARY Record 37;VAR ResLedgEntry_P@1000000006 : Record 203);
    VAR
      ResACPvalueEntry_L@1000000002 : Record 60131;
      NoSequence_L@1000000003 : Integer;
      ResACPAssignment_L@1000000004 : Record 60130;
      PurchACPLedgerEntry_L@1000000005 : Record 60122;
      ACPLedgerEntry_L@1000000007 : Record 60122;
      AmtRndPrecision_L@1000000010 : Decimal;
      GLSetup_L@1000000009 : Record 98;
      UnitAmtRndPrecision_L@1000000008 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // fonction permettant de cr'er les 'critures sp'cifique d'affectation ressources ACP
      //
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - SalesInvLine_P  :  la ligne de facture vente que le systSme vient de cr'er
      //   - TempSalesLine_P :  la ligne temp sales line de type facture non valid'e  utilis' par le systSme pour cr'er la facture valid'e
      //   - ResLedgEntry_P  :  l'enregistrement "'criture ressource" que le systSme vient de cr'er pour lal igne de facture associ'e
      //
      // Sorties:
      //   - Neant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      GLSetup_L.GET;
      AmtRndPrecision_L := GLSetup_L."Amount Rounding Precision";
      UnitAmtRndPrecision_L := GLSetup_L."Unit-Amount Rounding Precision";


      // --> 20200207-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01
      //on g'nSre nos 'critures assignation ressource ACP Sp'

      //les infos fondamentales (type doc, n› doc et n› ligne doc achat sont dans les 'critures ACP point'e)
      ResACPAssignment_L.RESET;
      ResACPAssignment_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",TempSalesLine."Document Type");
      ResACPAssignment_L.SETRANGE("Document No.",TempSalesLine."Document No.");
      ResACPAssignment_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.",TempSalesLine."Line No.");
      IF ResACPAssignment_L.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
        IF ResACPAssignment_L."Acp Ledger Entry No." <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          //cr'ation de l''criture en tant que telle
          PurchACPLedgerEntry_L.GET(ResACPAssignment_L."Acp Ledger Entry No.");

          NoSequence_L := 1;
          ResACPvalueEntry_L.LOCKTABLE;
          ResACPvalueEntry_L.RESET;
          IF ResACPvalueEntry_L.FINDLAST THEN
            NoSequence_L := ResACPvalueEntry_L."Entry No." + 1;

          ResACPvalueEntry_L.INIT;
          ResACPvalueEntry_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(PurchACPLedgerEntry_L);
          ResACPvalueEntry_L."Entry No." := NoSequence_L;
          // --> 20200626-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01
          {BEFORE
          ResACPvalueEntry_L."Resource No." := SalesInvLine_P."No.";
          AFTER}
          IF TempSalesLine."Document Type" = TempSalesLine."Document Type"::Invoice THEN
            ResACPvalueEntry_L."Resource No." := SalesInvLine_P."No."
          ELSE
            ResACPvalueEntry_L."Resource No." := SalesCrMemoLine_P."No.";
          // <-- 20200626-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01
          ResACPvalueEntry_L."Qty Affected on Res." := ResACPAssignment_L."Qty. to Assign";
          ResACPvalueEntry_L."Unit Cost Affected" := ResACPAssignment_L."Unit Cost";
          ResACPvalueEntry_L."Amount Affected" := ResACPAssignment_L."Amount to Assign";
          ResACPvalueEntry_L."Sales Document Type" := TempSalesLine_P."Document Type";

          IF TempSalesLine."Document Type" = TempSalesLine."Document Type"::Invoice THEN BEGIN
            ResACPvalueEntry_L."Sales Document No." := SalesInvLine_P."Document No.";
            ResACPvalueEntry_L."Sales Document Line No." := SalesInvLine_P."Line No.";
          END ELSE BEGIN
            ResACPvalueEntry_L."Sales Document No." := SalesCrMemoLine_P."Document No.";
            ResACPvalueEntry_L."Sales Document Line No." := SalesCrMemoLine_P."Line No.";
          END;

          ResACPvalueEntry_L."Res. Ledger Entry No." := ResLedgEntry_P."Entry No.";
          ResACPvalueEntry_L.INSERT;

          // Cr'ation de l''criture ACP ressource
          NoSequence_L := 1;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L.LOCKTABLE;
          IF ACPLedgerEntry_L.FINDLAST THEN
            NoSequence_L := ACPLedgerEntry_L."Entry No." + 1;

          ACPLedgerEntry_L.INIT;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(PurchACPLedgerEntry_L, FALSE);
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Entry No." := NoSequence_L;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"  := ACPLedgerEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Sale;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Source Type" := ACPLedgerEntry_L."Source Type"::Customer;

          ACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP  No. final doc. purch." := PurchACPLedgerEntry_L."Entry No.";

          IF TempSalesLine."Document Type" = TempSalesLine."Document Type"::Invoice THEN BEGIN
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Document Type" := ACPLedgerEntry_L."Document Type"::"ACP Sales Invoice";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Resource No."  := SalesInvLine_P."No.";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Document No."  := SalesInvLine_P."Document No.";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Document Line No." :=  SalesInvLine_P."Line No.";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Posting Date" := SalesInvLine_P."Posting Date";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."VAT Bus. Posting Group"  := SalesInvLine_P."VAT Prod. Posting Group";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."VAT Prod. Posting Group" := SalesInvLine_P."VAT Bus. Posting Group";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Location Code"           := SalesInvLine_P."Location Code";
          END ELSE BEGIN
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Document Type" := ACPLedgerEntry_L."Document Type"::"ACP Sales Credit Memo" ;
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Resource No."  := SalesCrMemoLine_P."No.";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Document No."  := SalesCrMemoLine_P."Document No.";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Document Line No." :=  SalesCrMemoLine_P."Line No.";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Posting Date" := SalesCrMemoLine_P."Posting Date";
            // --> 20200414-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01
            {BEFORE
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."VAT Bus. Posting Group"  := SalesCrMemoLine_P."VAT Prod. Posting Group";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."VAT Prod. Posting Group" := SalesCrMemoLine_P."VAT Bus. Posting Group";
            AFTER}
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."VAT Bus. Posting Group"  := SalesCrMemoLine_P."VAT Bus. Posting Group";
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."VAT Prod. Posting Group" := SalesCrMemoLine_P."VAT Prod. Posting Group";

            // <-- 20200414-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01
            ACPLedgerEntry_L."Location Code"           := SalesCrMemoLine_P."Location Code";
          END;

          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Final Doc ACP amount" := ROUND(ResACPAssignment_L."Amount to Assign", AmtRndPrecision_L);
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Invoiced Quantity"    := ResACPAssignment_L."Qty. to Assign";

          ACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP Amount (Invoice)"       := ROUND(ResACPAssignment_L."Amount to Assign", AmtRndPrecision_L);
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP Unit Amount (Invoice)"  := ResACPAssignment_L."Unit Cost";

          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Valuation Type" := ACPLedgerEntry_L."Valuation Type"::"Unit Amount";

          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Document Date"            := ResLedgEntry_P."Posting Date";
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Source Code"              := ResLedgEntry_P."Source Code";
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Gen. Bus. Posting Group"  := ResLedgEntry_P."Gen. Bus. Posting Group";
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Gen. Prod. Posting Group" := ResLedgEntry_P."Gen. Prod. Posting Group";
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."External Document No."    := ResLedgEntry_P."External Document No.";

          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Cost Amount (Actual)"       := 0;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Valuation Date"             := 0D;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Purchase Amount (Actual)"   := 0;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Purchase Amount (Expected)" := 0;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Sales Amount (Expected)"    := 0;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Cost Amount (Expected)"     := 0;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."Cost Amount (Non-Invtbl.)"  := 0;
          // --> 20200313-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01
          //suppression de la ligne ci-dessous
          //ACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP Code" := '';
          // <-- 20200313-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01
          ACPLedgerEntry_L.Rank       := 0;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP Unit Amount (Receipt)"  := 0;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP Amount (Receipt)"       := 0;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP Unit Am. to Invoice"    := 0;
          ACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP Unit Price Outstanding" := 0;

          ACPLedgerEntry_L.INSERT(TRUE);

          // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
          // Mise . jour du Prix unitaire APC restant . facturer
          // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
          PurchACPLedgerEntry_L.RESET;
          IF PurchACPLedgerEntry_L.GET(ACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP  No. final doc. purch.") THEN BEGIN
            IF PurchACPLedgerEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry No." = ACPLedgerEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry No." THEN BEGIN
              PurchACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP Unit Price Outstanding" :=
              PurchACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP Unit Price Outstanding" -
                ABS(ACPLedgerEntry_L."ACP Unit Amount (Invoice)");
              PurchACPLedgerEntry_L.MODIFY(FALSE);
            END;
          END;


        END;
      UNTIL ResACPAssignment_L.NEXT = 0;

      // <-- 20200207-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateJnlLineAcpPurchLd@1000000008();

            END;
        UNTIL PurchDocAcp_L.NEXT=0;

      // <-- 20200629-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateJnlLineCostPlusPurchLd@1000000009();
    VAR
      PurchDocCostPlus_L@1000000001 : Record 60121;
      CostPlusList_L@1000000000 : Record 60118;
    BEGIN
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Cette fonction est reprise du Cu90 pour g'rer la partie achat Cost Plus en LD dans ce cu80
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      // --> 20200629-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01

      CLEAR(PurchDocCostPlus_L);
      PurchDocCostPlus_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", PurchOrderLine."Document Type");
      PurchDocCostPlus_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", PurchOrderLine."Document No.");
      PurchDocCostPlus_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.", PurchOrderLine."Line No.");

      IF PurchDocCostPlus_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        REPEAT
            IF CostPlusList_L.GET(PurchDocCostPlus_L."Cost Plus Code") THEN BEGIN
            TempCMCJnlLineCostPlus_G."Table ID" := 39;
            TempCMCJnlLineCostPlus_G."Journal Template Name" := ItemJnlLine."Journal Template Name";
            TempCMCJnlLineCostPlus_G."Journal Batch Name" := ItemJnlLine."Journal Batch Name";
            TempCMCJnlLineCostPlus_G."Journal Line No." := ItemJnlLine."Line No.";

            TempCMCJnlLineCostPlus_G."Final Price" := PurchDocCostPlus_L."Final Price";
            TempCMCJnlLineCostPlus_G."Cost Plus Vendor No." := PurchDocCostPlus_L."Cost Plus vendor No.";
            TempCMCJnlLineCostPlus_G.INSERT(FALSE);
            END;
        UNTIL PurchDocCostPlus_L.NEXT=0;
